### Basic Savings Account

Basic Savings Account is a deposit account for small/ marginal/ landless/ natural disaster affected farmers and micro/ small traders who might need re-financing facility in future.
No minimum balance requirement
Opportunity to open account with only BDT 10
No Account Maintenance Fee
#### Features
Loan facility allowed with proper credit approval
Debit Card for easy cash withdrawal
Cash back facility on spending amount through …
Online banking service, SMS Alert & Call Center …

#### Eligibility
- 18 years or above Bangladeshi

- Applicant’s profession must be within the eligible small/micro income-generating activities as specified under Bangladesh Bank GBCSRD Circular No. 01/2014.

Eligible Professions / Activities

Small retail shop owners (grocery, stationery, clothing, book shops, etc.)
Hawkers, street vendors, and mobile sellers (including cloth vendors)
Tea stall and food stall operators
Tailors, garment workers, and small clothing service providers
Barbers and salon workers
Cobblers and small craftsmen (goldsmith, blacksmith, potter, etc.)
Electricians, mechanics, repair technicians (electrical/electronics)
Plumbers, carpenters, masons, rod workers, grill workers, painters
Rickshaw/van pullers and small transport workers
Fishermen and small-scale fish traders
Small-scale farmers (crop, vegetable cultivation, nursery, etc.)
Livestock and poultry farmers
Home-based food producers (snacks, bakery, pickle/pitha makers, etc.)
Handicraft, block/batik, and cottage industry workers
Flower sellers, newspaper vendors, and similar small traders
Mobile recharge, internet, and information service providers
and other similar income-generating activities as defined under Bangladesh Bank GBCSRD Circular No. 01/2014.

#### Required Document
NID/Birth certificate/Passport
Photograph – 2 Copies
Nominee’s NID/Birth certificate/Passport
Nominee photograph – 1 Copy
Testimonial certificate from competent authority on stating the occupation of the applicant. 
E-TIN (if applicable)

*Bank may ask for any additional document if required so.

Interest on end of day balance
Half yearly interest payment

#### FAQ
Q1. Who can open a Business Solution Account?
Any non-individual customer such as businesses, institutions, organizations, and professional entities eligible under Polygon Bank’s non-individual account opening requirements.

Q2. Is this account available for individuals?
No. It is strictly for non-individual entities.

Q3. What type of account is this?
It is an interest-bearing transactional current account designed for non-individual customers.

Q4. What currency is this account maintained in?
The account is maintained in BDT (Bangladeshi Taka).

Q5. Can an entity open more than one Business Solution Account?
No. Each entity can open only one Business Solution Account.

Q6. Is joint ownership allowed?
No. Joint ownership is not applicable for non-individual accounts.

Q7. Where can a customer open this account?
This account can be opened at any branch or sub-branch of Polygon Bank across the country.

Q8. What documents are required to open this account?
Please see the details here.

Q9. How is the account operated?
The account is operated by the authorized signatory(ies) of the entity as per board resolution, mandate, partnership letter, or equivalent authorization documents.

Q10. If a customer already maintains a non-individual Current, Easy, or Easy Plus Account with Polygon Bank, can it be converted to a Business Solution Account?
Yes. Existing non-individual accounts may be converted into a Business Solution Account, subject to fulfilling all required documentation and product conditions.

Q11. Is there any limit on deposits?
No. Deposit amount is unlimited, in line with Bangladesh Bank regulations.

Q12. What is the minimum opening balance?
BDT 50,000.

Q13. What balance is required to earn interest?
Interest is earned when the daily End-of-Day balance is BDT 50,000 or above.

Q14. How is interest calculated?
Interest is calculated based on the daily End-of-Day balance.

Q15. When is interest paid?
Interest is paid on a monthly basis.

Q16. What is the interest rate?
Interest rates are slab-based, approved by ALCO, and updated on Polygon Bank’s website.

Q17. Is a cheque book available?
Yes. The first cheque book is free. Additional cheque books are free if the last six months’ monthly average balance was BDT 50,000 or above; otherwise, SOC charges apply.

Q18. Is a debit card available?
Yes, but only for proprietorship concerns.

Q19. What are the debit card charges?
All debit card-related charges follow the Schedule of Charges (SOC).

Q20. Is PolygonTouch Digital Banking available?

Q21. Is PolygonCorp available?
Yes. PolygonCorp access is available. To know more about PolygonCorp click here

Q22. Is SMS Alert service available?
Yes. SMS Alert service is available, and charges (if applicable) follow the Schedule of Charges.

Q23. Is an e-statement available?
Yes. An e-statement is available.

Q24. Is customer support available?
Yes. Customer support is available.

Q25. Is account maintenance fee applicable?
Yes. If the customer maintains a monthly average balance of BDT 50,000 or above during the last six months, no account maintenance fee will be charged. Otherwise, an account maintenance fee of BDT 500 will be charged on a half-yearly basis.

Q26. Are intercity cash transactions free?
Yes. Intercity cash deposits and withdrawals are free.

Q27. What is the account closing fee?
BDT 300.

Q28. Is excise duty applicable?
Yes. Excise duty is applied as per prevailing government regulations.

Q29. Is source tax applicable?
Yes. Source tax is deducted as per prevailing tax regulations.

#### FAQ
1. What is the Freelancer Deposit Product of Polygon Bank?

The Freelancer Deposit Product is a bundled account solution designed for Bangladeshi freelancers to receive, retain, and utilize foreign inward remittances in compliance with the prevailing regulations and guidelines of Bangladesh Bank. The product facilitates the formal banking of freelancing income, including incentive-eligible foreign remittances, through a structured combination of local and foreign currency accounts.

2. Where can customers open the Freelancer Deposit Product accounts?

Customers may open the Freelancer Deposit Product at all branches and sub-branches of Polygon Bank across Bangladesh, subject to fulfillment of applicable documentation, verification, and regulatory requirements.

3. What accounts are included in the Freelancer Deposit Product?

The Freelancer Deposit Product consists of the following two accounts:
a) Freelancer Savings Account (BDT), which is an interest-bearing savings account maintained in local currency.
b) Freelancer ERQ Account (USD), which is a non-interest-bearing foreign currency current account.

4.Who is eligible to avail the Freelancer Deposit Product?

Any Bangladeshi individual who is at least 18 years of age, resides in Bangladesh, and holds a valid Freelancer ID is eligible to avail the Freelancer Deposit Product, subject to compliance with Bangladesh Bank regulations and Polygon Bank’s internal policies.

5. What documents are required to open the Freelancer Deposit Product?

To open the Freelancer Deposit Product with Polygon Bank, the following documents are required, subject to regulatory verification and the Bank’s internal compliance requirements:
For the account holder:
•    Valid photo identification document (NID/Birth certificate/Passport)
•    Two recent passport-size photographs
•    Valid Freelancer ID
For the nominee:
•    Valid photo identification document
•    One recent passport-size photograph
All documents must be valid, complete, and acceptable to the Bank as per prevailing regulations.

6. What is a Freelancer ID card and where can a customer obtain it?

A Freelancer ID is an official identification that certifies an individual’s status as a freelancer. Freelancer IDs issued by the Government of Bangladesh through the ICT Division are accepted for availing the Freelancer Deposit Product. In addition, Freelancer ID cards issued by the Bangladesh Freelancer Development Society (BFDS) are also accepted, subject to verification and regulatory compliance.

7. Is joint operation allowed for Freelancer accounts?

No. Both the Freelancer Savings Account and the Freelancer ERQ Account are strictly maintained as single-operation individual accounts. Joint operation is not permitted under this product.

8. Is there any minimum balance requirement to open the accounts?

No initial deposit or minimum opening balance is required to open either the Freelancer Savings Account or the Freelancer ERQ Account.

9. How is interest calculated on the Freelancer Savings Account?

Interest on the Freelancer Savings Account is calculated on a EOD balance and credited monthly, in accordance with the prevailing interest rate approved by ALCO. Interest is payable only if the end-of-day balance is at least BDT 10,000, subject to applicable regulations.

10. What is the interest rate of the Freelancer Savings Account?

The interest rate applicable to the Freelancer Savings Account is determined by Polygon Bank in line with its prevailing deposit rate structure and may be revised from time to time. The latest and applicable interest rates for the Freelancer Savings Account are published on Polygon Bank’s official website and can be accessed through the following link:
[Polygon Bank website]

11. Does Freelancer ERQ Account earn any interest?

No. The Freelancer ERQ Account is a non-interest-bearing foreign currency current account and does not earn any interest.

12. What is an ERQ account?

ERQ stands for Exporters’ Retention Quota. An ERQ account is a foreign currency account permitted under Bangladesh Bank regulations that allows eligible exporters, including freelancers, to retain a prescribed portion of their inward foreign remittances in foreign currency for approved purposes.

13. What are the benefits of the Freelancer ERQ Account?

The Freelancer ERQ Account allows freelancers to retain a portion of eligible inward foreign remittances in foreign currency in accordance with Bangladesh Bank regulations. Funds held in the ERQ account may be used for bona fide business expenses and permissible personal expenses through approved banking channels. The balance may also be used through an international debit card for eligible foreign currency transactions or converted into local currency at an Authorized Dealer branch at the prevailing exchange rate, subject to regulatory requirements.

14. Are all freelancing income funds retained in the Freelancer ERQ Account?

No. For inward remittances received from freelancing platforms approved by Bangladesh Bank under FE Circular No. 07 dated 30 January 2022, a prescribed portion may be retained in the ERQ Account, while the remaining portion is encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account. Freelancing income is not retained in full in the Freelancer ERQ Account. Retention is permitted only up to the portion prescribed by Bangladesh Bank, while the remaining amount is mandatorily encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account in local currency.

15. What happens to freelancing income received from different websites or platforms?

All freelancing income is initially credited to the Freelancer ERQ Account. For inward remittances received from freelancing platforms approved by Bangladesh Bank under FE Circular No. 07 dated 30 January 2022, a prescribed portion may be retained in the ERQ Account, while the remaining portion is encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account. Freelancing income received from other websites or platforms that are not listed as Bangladesh Bank-approved platforms is fully encashed and transferred to the linked Freelancer Savings Account, with no retention permitted in the ERQ Account.

16. What conversion rate is applied when funds are transferred to the Freelancer Savings Account?

The prevailing exchange rate of the day is applied when foreign currency funds are transferred from the Freelancer ERQ Account to the linked Freelancer Savings Account. The daily exchange rate is published on the official website of Polygon Bank.

17. What portion of freelancing income can be retained in the Freelancer ERQ Account?

As per prevailing Bangladesh Bank regulations, currently 35 percent of eligible freelancing income may be retained in the Freelancer ERQ Account. The remaining 65 percent is encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account in local currency. This ratio is subject to change based on future regulatory directives.

18. How can funds in the Freelancer Savings Account be used?

Funds in the Freelancer Savings Account may be used in the same manner as a regular savings account, including deposits, withdrawals, and fund transfers, subject to the laws of Bangladesh and applicable regulations. The associated debit card may be endorsed for foreign currency usage and used for permissible international transactions.

19. Can a customer open Fixed Deposit or DPS from the Freelancer Savings Account?

Yes. A customer may open term deposit products such as Fixed Deposit or DPS using funds from the Freelancer Savings Account, in the same manner as applicable for regular Savings or Current Accounts. Such term deposits will be governed by Polygon Bank’s standard product terms, conditions, and prevailing policies.

20. Are debit cards available with the Freelancer accounts?

Yes. A dual-currency debit card is available with the Freelancer Savings Account, and the first debit card issued under the product is provided free of charge for lifetime. An international debit card is available with the Freelancer ERQ Account for permissible foreign currency transactions. A valid passport is required to avail this card, and the card is also issued free of charge for lifetime.

21. Are cheque books available for Freelancer accounts?

Cheque book facilities are available for the Freelancer Savings Account only. Cheque books are not available for the Freelancer ERQ Account.

22. Are PolygonTouch and SMS services available for Freelancer accounts?

Yes. PolygonTouch internet banking and SMS alert services are available for both Freelancer accounts. PolygonTouch functionality for the Freelancer ERQ Account is limited. SMS charges for the Freelancer Savings Account apply as per the prevailing Schedule of Charges, while SMS service for the Freelancer ERQ Account is provided free of charge.

23. Will tax on interest and excise duties be applied to the Freelancer accounts?

Yes. Applicable taxes, excise duties, VAT, and other statutory charges are applied in accordance with the prevailing laws and regulations of the Government of Bangladesh and directives issued by Bangladesh Bank. Any tax on interest earned is deducted at source as per applicable regulations.

24. Are remittance certificates provided to Freelancer customers?

Yes. Four remittance certificates are issued free of charge each year on a quarterly basis and are sent to the customer’s registered mailing address.

Source: https://www.citybankplc.com/p/freelancer-solution

Please click here to find the interest rate
#### Premature Encashment
For accounts opened after July 2018:
- For less than 36 months, the account holder gets the self-deposited amount and no interest is allowed.
- For more than 36 months, the customer gets the DPS rate for the completed tenor. For any broken month or days, the customer gets the savings rate.

For accounts opened before July 2018:
- For less than 1 year, the account holder only gets the self-deposited amount.
- For more than 1 year but less than 3 years, the account holder receives the self-deposited amount along with interest at the prevailing General Savings Account interest rate.
- For more than 3 years but less than 5 years, the account holder receives the matured value of 3 years along with the self-deposited amount, plus interest at the prevailing General Savings Account interest rate for the fraction period.
- For more than 5 years but less than 7 years, the account holder receives the matured value of 5 years along with the self-deposited amount, plus interest at the prevailing General Savings Account interest rate for the fraction period.
- For more than 7 years but less than 10 years, the account holder receives the matured value of 7 years along with the self-deposited amount, plus interest at the prevailing General Savings Account interest rate for the fraction period.

#### FAQ
1. What is the Insurance-Backed DPS?

The Insurance-Backed DPS is a monthly deposit scheme where a customer deposits a fixed amount for a selected tenure, and in the event of the depositor’s death during the DPS period, the nominee receives the full insured amount as per the policy.

2. Who can open an Insurance-Backed DPS?

Any Bangladeshi citizen aged 18 to 55 years who maintains a Savings or Current Deposit account with Polygon Bank is eligible to open this DPS.

3. Where can the account be opened?

The account can be opened at any branch or sub-branch of Polygon Bank.

4. What tenures are available?

Tenures of 5, 10, 15, and 20 years are available. The maximum tenor depends on the age of the applicant as well.

Tenor

	

Maximum Age of the Customer

5 Years

	

Below 55 Years

10 Years

	

Below 50 Years

15 Years

	

Below 45 Years

20 Years

	

Below 40 Years

 

5. What is the monthly installment amount?

The monthly installment amount can range from BDT 500 to BDT 10,000 and must be in multiples of BDT 500.

6. Is insurance coverage included?

Yes. The DPS includes insurance coverage from the date of opening, and the bank pays the insurance premium on behalf of the customer.

7. What happens if the account holder dies during the DPS period?

If the account holder dies during the DPS period, the nominee receives the full matured amount, and the DPS cannot be continued by the nominee.

8. Who can be assigned as the nominee?

The nominee must be the account holder’s parent, spouse, or child. If the nominee is a minor, a legal guardian must also be assigned.

9. Can multiple nominees be added?

Yes.

10. Can the nominee be changed later?

Yes. The nominee may be changed at any time before maturity by submitting a written instruction to the bank.

 

 

11. How are monthly installments collected?

Installments are collected through auto-debit from the customer’s linked Savings or Current Deposit account. Cash cannot be deposited directly into the DPS account.

12. What happens if a monthly installment is missed?

If an installment is missed, charges will be applied according to the Schedule of Charges. If three consecutive installments remain unpaid, the DPS will be closed automatically after notifying the customer.

13. Can a customer open more than one Insurance-Backed DPS?

Yes. Customers may open multiple DPS accounts under this scheme.

14. Is a loan facility available against this DPS?

Yes. After completing one year of the DPS, a customer may obtain a loan up to 90% of the deposited amount.

15. What happens if the account is closed before maturity?

If the account is closed within the first year, the customer will receive only the deposited amount; no interest will be paid, and the insurance premium paid by the bank will be deducted. If the account is closed after one year but before maturity, the customer will receive the deposited amount with savings interest, and the insurance premium paid by the bank will be deducted.

16. Under what circumstances is a medical examination mandatory?

A medical examination is required when the maturity value exceeds BDT 7,50,000, or when the customer’s age exceeds 50 years.

17. What types of medical examinations are required?

18. Is there a designated list of hospitals?

No. Customers may complete medical tests at any reputed hospital or any renowned diagnostic center in Bangladesh.

19. When should customers submit their medical reports?

Medical reports must be submitted at the time of opening the DPS.

20. Who will pay for the required medical tests?

The customer will initially pay for the medical tests. Once the DPS is opened and reports are submitted, the insurance company will reimburse the expenses as per the approved reimbursement rates.

21. Is there a reimbursement limit for medical examination expenses?

Yes. The reimbursement limits are as follows:

22. What fees or duties apply?

Applicable taxes, excise duty, and regulatory charges will be deducted as per rules.

23. Are cheque books or passbooks issued?

No. Cheque books and passbooks are not issued for this DPS.

24. Can cash be deposited directly into the DPS account?

No. All installments must be collected through auto-debit from the linked account.

25. Within what timeframe must the nominee notify the bank in the event of the account holder’s death?

In the event of the account holder’s death, the nominee must inform the bank within 90 days from the date of death. If the bank is not notified within this stipulated period, the insurance benefit under the scheme will not be applicable.

#### Who can open a Mariners FCY Account?
A Bangladeshi citizen, minimum 18 years of age, employed as a seafarer by a foreign shipping company earning in foreign currency.

#### Can a seafarer working for a Bangladeshi shipping company open this account?
No. This account is only for Bangladeshi seafarers employed by foreign shipping companies.

#### Can a foreign national open this account?
No. Only Bangladeshi citizens are eligible.

#### What type of account is Mariners FCY Account?
It is an Individual Current Account maintained in USD.

#### What documents are required to open the account?
Recent passport photo, NID/Passport/Birth Certificate, Nominee ID and photo, Utility bill (within 6 months) or parent/spouse utility copy with proof, Seaman’s Book/SRB/CDC/Discharge Book, Employment proof with joining date or contract.

#### Is there any minimum opening balance?
No, there is no minimum balance requirement.

#### In which branches can the account be opened?
At any Polygon Bank branch or sub-branch across Bangladesh.

#### Is joint account allowed?
No, joint account is not permitted.

#### Can mandate be assigned?
Yes, mandate assignment is allowed

#### What funds can be credited into this account?
Self-earned remittance, wage earner remittance, and foreign currency brought into Bangladesh (above USD 10,000 must be declared via customs FMJ form).

#### What funds cannot be credited?
Foreign exchange earned from business or services rendered within Bangladesh.

#### Is there any deposit limit?
No, deposit is unlimited as per applicable regulations.

#### Can an existing FCY account be converted?
Yes, if eligible. Customer must submit: i) Seaman’s Book/SRB/CDC/Discharge Book ii) Employer/agent contract or letter confirming next joining or current engagement.

#### Is cheque book available?
Yes, 1st cheque book is free. Subsequent books are chargeable as per SOC.

#### Is debit card available?
Yes, International Polygon Mastercard Debit Card is available and lifetime free for FCY account holders.

#### Can the debit card be used abroad?
Yes, it can be used overseas.

#### Are intercity cash transactions chargeable?
No, intercity cash deposit and withdrawal are free.

#### Is PolygonTouch available?
Yes, PolygonTouch digital banking is available.

#### Is SMS Alert available?
Yes, SMS Alert service is free of charge.

#### Is account maintenance fee applicable?
No, account maintenance fee is fully waived.

#### Is this an interest-bearing account?
No. This account does not offer interest on the deposited balance.

#### Are there any locker benefits?
Yes. A 50% waiver on the first year’s locker charge is offered. However, locker facilities are subject to availability at the respective branch.

#### Is student file opening fee discounted?
Yes. 50% waiver on child’s student file opening fee applies.

#### Eligibility
18 years or above Bangladeshi mariners or seafarers working for foreign shipping companies and salary credited in FCY.

#### Frequently Asked Questions
Account Opening

1. What is Resident Foreign Currency Deposit (RFCD) account?

Ans: RFCD is a Deposit account in foreign currency opened and maintained by resident Bangladeshis with the foreign exchange brought in at the time of their return from travel abroad. Only Resident Bangladeshis can open this account.

2. Who can open RFCD account?

Ans: Any Bangladeshi resident aged over 18 years can open Resident Foreign Currency Deposit (RFCD) account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 21)

3. Can a foreign national open an RFCD account?

Ans: No. RFCD account is for only resident Bangladeshis. However, foreign nationals can open other foreign currency accounts. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 21)

4. What are the required documents to open an RFCD account?

Ans: The required documents are:

Passport size photograph -2 copies of account holder, 1 copy of nominee
Valid Bangladeshi Passport (information page)
PSR or Proof of Submission of Return – tax return of previous financial year mandatory for balance equivalent to BDT 10 lacs or more
Nominee photo ID – NID /Birth certificate/Passport

Note: At the time of depositing any cash foreign currency into the account, we will need the original valid Bangladeshi Passport to check the travel dates i.e., Arrival seal pages of the journey against which deposit is being made. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

5. What is the account type of RFCD account?

Ans: It is an interest-bearing deposit account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 25)

6. Can a customer open a joint RFCD account?

Ans: No, a customer cannot open a joint RFCD account, but the customer can provide two supplementary debit or credit card to his dependents against the account. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

7. Can customer add nominee to the RFCD account?

Ans: Yes, nominee(s) can be added to the RFCD account.

8. Is it possible to open/deposit RFCD account if the passport is expired?

Ans: No. Current valid passport is required to open or make any deposit to RFCD account. Additionally, proof of foreign travel i.e., the visa and arrival seal on the valid passport is required to be submitted for any credit.

9. In which currencies can an individual open an RFCD account in Polygon Bank?

Ans: Currently, we can open RFCD account in USD, GBP & EURO. i.e. U.S Dollar, Pound & Euro (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December, 03, 2023)

10. Can customer open RFCD account in any branch?

Ans: Customers can place account opening request at any of our branches.  However, the account will be opened at an AD branch of the bank.  (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

11. What is the minimum account opening balance of RFCD account?

Ans: USD 500 or equivalent. This, however, can be waived with proper approvals at branch level. 

12. Can a customer open multiple RFCD accounts in a bank?

Ans: Yes, a customer can open multiple RFCD accounts but in different currencies in a bank.

13. Can a Non-resident Bangladeshi open RFCD account?

Ans: No. Only resident Bangladeshi individual is allowed to open an RFCD account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 21)

14. Is it possible to open RFCD account without foreign travel?

Ans: Yes. But in this case, no deposits will be allowed at the time of account opening. Any deposit requires proof of foreign travel. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

Account Operations

1. What are the options of deposit in the RFCD account?

Ans: A customer can deposit Cash foreign currency in the RFCD account.

Cash foreign currency Deposit 

Any amount up to USD 10,000 or equivalent per trip can be deposited without any declaration. However, if the travel took place before 03-02-2020, the amount is USD 5,000 per trip. 

If the amount is above USD 10,000 per trip, then a declaration to Customs Authorities in Form FMJ while entering the country, will be required to be submitted and the deposit must be made within 30 days of arrival. (Bangladesh Bank Press release DCP(PR)05/2022-301)

In all cases of deposit, customers must fill out Polygon Bank’s RFCD declaration form mentioning the travel details.

2. Is there any time limit for depositing cash foreign currency in RFCD Account?

Ans: No, opening an RFCD account does not have to be related to any travel.  A customer can open RFCD account any time with a current valid passport. But to credit any fund against any travel, the travel must be undertaken with the current valid passport.  The bank will not credit any funds against a travel which was undertaken by the customer previously and that particular passport has expired. 

However, foreign currency has to be deposited within 30 days of arrival for deposits which require declaration on Form FMJ as stated in question no.1 above. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

3. What is an FMJ form?

Ans: FMJ form is a 3-page Customs Declaration Form for bringing cash foreign currency above USD 10,000 into the country. It must be availed and declared at the airport while crossing Customs check.

4. Is cash foreign currency endorsement from RFCD Account a part of Yearly Travel Quota?

Ans: No. Cash currency endorsement limit from RFCD Account and Yearly Travel Quota are totally separate. 

5. How much can the foreign cash currency be endorsed from RFCD account?

Ans: Maximum cash of USD 5,000 can be endorsed from an RFCD Account per person per trip. If customer wishes, they can also cash endorse the remaining RFCD Account balance in other currency (subject to availability). (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 22)

6. What is the limit of endorsement of other foreign cash currency (not USD) from RFCD account?

Ans: There is no limit. The total available balance in RFCD Account can be taken in cash in foreign currencies other than USD (subject to availability) against endorsement while traveling. 

7. Can a customer endorse cash USD 5,000 from their RFCD Account and in addition endorse USD 5,000 from their yearly Travel Quota (a total of cash USD 10,000) for the same trip?

Ans: No, in that case the customer can avail either one for each trip. Meaning, the maximum limit is USD 5,000 cash currency endorsement per trip. He can avail from both the entitlements but not in the same trip. 

8. Can a customer endorse their passport in a different currency other than the currency of their RFCD account?

Ans: Yes, a customer can do that. Meaning, if the RFCD account is in USD, he can still endorse in equivalent EUR or any other currency he prefers (subject of availability). 

9. Is it possible to endorse cash foreign currency to the account holder’s dependent’s passport prior to foreign travel?

No. Cash endorsement can be used for account holder’s own travel purposes only.

10. Can a customer withdraw cash from an ATM abroad during a foreign travel from RFCD balance?

Ans: Yes. Customers can withdraw balance from an ATM abroad during a foreign travel.

11. Is there any withdrawal limit for ATMs during foreign travel?

Ans: No, there is no limit from Polygon Bank’s side.  There may be amount restrictions of the foreign bank whose ATM the customer is using. 

12. Is the balance in these accounts freely transferrable abroad?

Ans: Yes. Balances in the RFCD account is freely transferrable abroad. Account holders and their dependents can use it for the purpose of foreign travel, paying tuition fees, medical expenses etc.  (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 22)

13. If the customer has an RFCD account in one currency, can he/she send outward remittance in other currency?

Ans: Yes. In this case, currency conversion will take place. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 22)

14. Is it possible to withdraw BDT from ATM machine using RFCD debit/credit cards?

Ans: Yes. Currently it is possible to withdraw BDT from ATM machines using RFCD debit/credit cards.

15. Is it possible to withdraw and endorse cash foreign currency without upcoming foreign travel?

Ans: No that is not possible. Valid Visa along with travel ticket (travel date within the next 14 days) is mandatory for endorsement. 

16. Can customers transfer the balance of one of their RFCD Account to another of their RFCD Account?

Ans: Balance of RFCD account cannot be transferred to another RFCD account of the same account holder within Polygon Bank or within any other bank.  

17. Can customers transfer the balance of any other FCY Account to their RFCD Account?

Ans: Balance of Foreign Currency account (FCY) cannot be transferred to RFCD account. 

18. What are eligible deposit/credit/inward transactions for RFCD Account?

Ans: RFCD account can only be credited with cash after returning from foreign travel or transfer from other RFCD account of the same customer. However, proceeds of export of goods or services from Bangladesh or commission arising from business deals in Bangladesh cannot be credited to such accounts.  (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

19. Can customers avail any credit card or debit card against RFCD account?

Ans: Customers can avail credit card and debit card against the account. Moreover, dependents of the customer can avail maximum of two supplementary credit card and debit card in their names. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

20. Is cheque book facility available in RFCD account?

Ans: Cheque book is not available in RFCD account.

21. Is it possible to receive remittance in RFCD account?

Remittance is not allowed in RFCD account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

22. In which purpose the RFCD account balance can be used?

Ans: RFCD account balance can be used to remit outside for the purpose of travelling abroad, education expenditure, medical expenditure and any other purpose.  

The entire balance held in RFCD Account can be easily converted to BDT. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

23. Can the balance be used for e-commerce transactions and is there any limit on transactions?

Ans: Yes. Balance of RFCD accounts can be used for e-commerce transactions. There is no cap or maximum celling for e-commerce transactions. (FE Circular Letter No. 05, May, 09, 2024)

24. Can a customer deposit multiple times against the same travel?

Ans: Yes, a customer can deposit multiple times against the same travel but within the permissible limit per trip. 

25. Can a customer open a Fixed Deposit with the balance of RFCD account?

Ans: No, a customer cannot open a Fixed Deposit with the balance of RFCD account.

26. Are there any supplementary cards available?

Ans: Yes, a maximum total of two supplementary cards (Debit or Credit) can be availed by dependents of the account holder. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

27. What Credit Cards can customers avail against RFCD account?

Ans: Customers can open RFCD Backed Credit Card and Zero Limit Credit Card (where balance can be transferred from account)

28. What is an RFCD Backed Credit Card?

Ans: Customer’s preferred RFCD account balance will be placed under “Lien”, and that equivalent balance will be available as limit for the RFCD Backed Credit Card. Essentially, works like a secured credit card.

29. What is a Zero Limit Credit Card?

Ans: Customer’s preferred RFCD account balance will be transferred to the Zero Limit Credit Card. Balance will be available as the limit for the Card. Essentially, works like a prepaid credit card.

Interest Rate

1. Is the RFCD account an interest-bearing account?

Ans: Yes, RFCD is an Interest-bearing account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 25) 

2. What is the interest rate and how will the customer know the prevailing interest rate?

Ans: The interest rate is decided by Polygon Bank and found in bank’s website at the following link: [Polygon Bank website] \

(FE Circular No 28, Dated October 31, 2024)

3. Is interest rate fixed in RFCD account?

Ans: Ans: No. The interest rate is not fixed. Polygon Bank may update the rate from time to time.

4. How frequently is the interest calculated?

Ans: Interest is calculated daily on daily end of day account balance.

5. How frequent is the interest paid?

Ans: Interest is credited quarterly in customer’s account.

6. Are there any interest eligibility criteria for RFCD account?

Ans: Yes. If the account balance of a customer goes below GPB 500, USD 1,000 or equivalent currency for another currency account in any single day, customer will not be eligible to earn interest for that month.

Equivalent currency of USD 1,000 for EURO will be determined through mid-rate conversion.

 

 

Tax & Excise Duty

1. Is tax applicable on the interest of RFCD account?

Ans: Yes. Tax is applicable and is deducted at Source (TDS) at the time of credit to the account. 

2. How much is the TDS deducted?

Ans: At the rate of 10% if the customer has submitted PSR (Proof of Submission of Return) for the previous financial year to the bank, otherwise, at the rate of 15%. 

3. Is Excise Duty applicable?

Ans: Yes, Excise Duty is applicable on RFCD account balance.

#### Frequently Asked Question
What is the Student Savings Account – College Plan?
It is a savings account designed for Bangladeshi students aged between 18 and 25 years. The account provides regular banking, transaction and savings facilities suitable for college and university students.

Who is eligible to open this account?
Bangladeshi students aged between 18 and 25 years are eligible to open a Student Savings Account – College Plan, subject to submission of the required documents.

Can the account be opened as a single account?
Yes. The account may be opened as a single account in the student’s name.

Can the account be opened jointly?
Yes. The account may be opened jointly by the student with a parent or another family member.

Is any minimum opening balance required?
No. There is no minimum balance requirement for opening the account.

What documents are required from the student?
The following documents are generally required:

Student’s NID, birth certificate, valid passport or driving licence
Recent photograph
Student ID card or tuition fee slip issued within the last six months
Applicable source-of-fund documents

Where the student has only a birth certificate without a photograph and does not have any valid photo ID, a certification letter from the relevant educational institution must be submitted. The student’s photograph must be attested by the principal, head teacher or an authorized official of the institution.

Are documents required from a joint account holder or beneficial owner?
Yes. Information and identification documents of the joint account holder or beneficial owner must be provided where applicable.

Additional documents, including source-of-fund documents, may also be required in accordance with the Bank’s KYC requirements.

Is a debit card available with the account?
Yes. Debit card facility is available with the Student Savings Account – College Plan.

What is the debit card charge?
The debit card is free for the first year. From the second year onward, a 50% waiver will apply to the regular debit card charge.

Is a cheque book available with the account?
Yes. Cheque book facility is available with the account.

What is the cheque book charge?
A 50% waiver will apply to the regular cheque book charge.

Is PolygonTouch available with the account?
Yes. PolygonTouch facility is available in accordance with the Bank’s applicable digital banking policy.

Is SMS Alert available?
Yes. SMS Alert facility is available and is free of charge.

What are the deposit and withdrawal limits?
Deposit and withdrawal limits will be determined based on the student’s or beneficial owner’s KYC profile and Transaction Profile, or TP, in accordance with the Bank’s applicable policy.

Can Term Deposit be opened from this account?
Yes. DPS, FDR and Monthly Interest Paying FD may be opened from the Student Savings Account – College Plan, subject to the Bank’s applicable product terms.

Is nomination facility available?
Yes. Nomination facility is available in accordance with the Bank’s prevailing nomination policy.

Can a student maintain more than one College Plan account?
No. A student may maintain only one Student Savings Account – College Plan with Polygon Bank.

Can a School Plan account be converted into a College Plan account?
Yes. Once the student becomes eligible, an existing Student Savings Account – School Plan may be converted into a Student Savings Account – College Plan after completion of the required KYC and account regularization process.

Can a student open a new College Plan account while maintaining a School Plan account?
No. The existing School Plan account must first be converted or closed before a new Student Savings Account – College Plan can be opened.

What happens when the account holder reaches 25 years of age?
The account will be placed under debit freeze when the account holder reaches 25 years of age.

The account holder must visit Polygon Bank Branch or Sub Branch to regularize or close the account. To regularize the account, the account holder must complete the required full KYC process.

After regularization, the account may be converted into another eligible savings account.

#### Eligibility
Bangladeshi student 18 to 25 years old

*Bank may ask for any additional document if required so.

### Student Savings Account (School Plan)

Student Savings Account School Plan
A joint account to secure the future of your children where we joining with you to fulfill the dream.
No Account Maintenance Fee
No Minimum Balance requirement
Interest on end of day balance
#### Features
Free Passport Endorsement
Free SMS Alert

#### Frequently Asked Question
What is the Student Savings Account – School Plan?
It is a savings account designed for Bangladeshi students below 18 years of age. The account helps students develop savings habits and access basic banking services from an early age.

Who is eligible to open this account?
Any Bangladeshi student below 18 years of age is eligible to open a Student Savings Account – School Plan.

Who will operate the account?
As the student is below 18 years of age, the account will be operated by the student’s parent or legal guardian in accordance with the Bank’s applicable procedures.

Is any minimum opening balance required?
No. There is no minimum balance requirement for opening the account.

What documents are required from the student?
The following documents are generally required:

Student’s NID, birth certificate or valid passport
Recent photograph
Student ID card or tuition fee slip issued within the last six months

Where the student has only a birth certificate without a photograph and does not have any valid photo ID, a certification letter from the relevant educational institution must be submitted. The student’s photograph must be attested by the principal, head teacher or an authorized official of the institution.

What documents are required from the parent or legal guardian?
The parent or legal guardian must generally provide:

Valid NID, passport or driving license
Recent photograph
Applicable source-of-fund documents

What is the monthly deposit limit?
According to Student Banking Guidelines of Bangladesh Bank, maximum of BDT 25,000 may be deposited into the account in a month.

What is the maximum account balance?
According to Student Banking Guidelines of Bangladesh Bank, the account balance must not exceed BDT 300,000 at any time.

Is a debit card available with the account?
Yes. A debit card may be issued in the student’s name.

What is the debit card transaction limit?
According to Student Banking Guidelines of Bangladesh Bank, the standard monthly transaction limit is BDT 15,000 for withdrawals through ATM, POS and permitted online channels.

The limit may be increased to a maximum of BDT 25,000 upon the request of the parent or legal guardian, subject to the Bank’s approval and applicable procedures.

Can debit card be issued in the parent’s or guardian’s name?
Debit card will be issued in the name of the parent or legal guardian. The debit card facility will be provided to the eligible student account holder in accordance with the Bank’s policy.

Is a cheque book available with the account?
No. Cheque book facility is not available with the Student Savings Account – School Plan.

Is PolygonTouch available with the account?
No. PolygonTouch facility is not available with this account.

Is SMS Alert available?
Yes. SMS alerts will be sent to the registered mobile number of the student’s parent or legal guardian.

Can a DPS be opened from this account?
Yes. A Deposit Pension Scheme, or DPS, may be opened from the Student Savings Account – School Plan, subject to the Bank’s applicable product terms.

What is the maximum monthly DPS instalment amount?
The monthly DPS instalment may be up to BDT 20,000, subject to the Bank’s applicable product rules.

Can an FDR or Monthly Interest Paying FD be opened from this account?
No. Only DPS may be opened from the Student Savings Account – School Plan.

Who can be nominated for the account?
Only the student’s parent or legal guardian may be nominated for the account.

Can a student maintain more than one School Plan account?
No. A student may maintain only one Student Savings Account – School Plan with Polygon Bank.

What happens when the student reaches 18 years of age?
The account will be placed under debit freeze when the student reaches 18 years of age.

The account holder must visit Polygon Bank Branch or Sub Branch to regularize or close the account. To regularize the account, the account holder must provide an updated identification document and recent photograph and complete the required full KYC process.

After regularization, the account may be converted into a Student Savings Account – College Plan or another eligible savings account.

#### Eligibility
Bangladeshi student below 18 years old
Parents or legal guardian of student as joint applicant (Must be Bangladeshi)

Student ID card or testimony from educational institution
Photograph – 2 Copies of student
NID/Birth certificate/Passport of joint applicant
Photograph – 2 Copies of joint applicant
Nominee’s NID/Birth certificate/Passport
Nominee photograph – 1 Copy
Income source document of joint applicant
E-TIN of joint applicant (if applicable)

*Bank may ask for any additional document if required so.

### PolygonGo

Rediscover Freedom
Welcome to the world of PolygonGo. All new PolygonGo. Live at your own space. Go wherever you want to, move as you wish to, enjoy life as you dream to.
Go wherever you want to, enjoy life as you dream to
2% Cashback on All Spending*
Pay wherever you want and enjoy 2% cashback
Flexibuy at 0% Interest
Buy now pay later with flexibuy at 0% interest
Up to BDT 5,000
Just Tap & Pay and get up to BDT 5,000
International Payment Facilities
Explore the world and pay hassle-free with PolygonGo

#### Who are eligible for a PolygonGo?
Customers with a Polygon Bank consumer VISA Credit Card or Current/Savings account are eligible to apply for a PolygonGo.
Note: Foreign Currency cards or Foreign Currency accounts are not eligible.

#### What is PolygonGo?
PolygonGo is the latest payment technology chip from Polygon Bank. The PolygonGo chip can be inserted into the Wristband or Strap loop to easily make NFC payments with a tap.

#### How can someone apply for a PolygonGo?
Customers can apply for a PolygonGo through our branches, card service centers or sales channel.

#### What are the color choices for the Wristbands?
The Wristband can be chosen from three colors; Black, Green and Orange.
Note: Color choices are subject to availability.

#### What are the color choices for the Strap loop?
The Strap loop is available in black color only.

#### Can the Wristband or Strap loop be purchased separately?
No. The Wristband and Strap loop has to be purchased together. Customer can use either according to his/her convenience

#### How to use a PolygonGo?
Customers can start using a PolygonGo by following these simple steps:

Step 1: Remove PolygonGo chip from the chip holder.
Step 2: Activate the PolygonGo chip and generate PIN through Smart IVR/IVR/PolygonTouch Digital Banking.
Step 3: Insert the PolygonGo chip into your Wristband or Strap loop.
Step 4: The PolygonGo device is now ready to make NFC payments.

#### What is included in the PolygonGo box?
The box includes:

NFC Wearable Chip
Chip Holder
Wristband
Strap loop
Mailer

#### Why use a PolygonGo?
With PolygonGo you can instantly make NFC payments with one tap. Morover, you can wear it on your wrist or pair it with your watch strap. PolygonGo is a blend of convenience and style making your purchases easier with each transaction.

#### How to do online transactions with your PolygonGo?
Information provided on the chip holder can be used to make online transactions with the PolygonGo.

#### Is there any transaction limit for the PolygonGo?
Maximum 20 NFC transactions can be made per day using your PolygonGo chip.

#### Is there any cashback offer on transactions with the PolygonGo?
Yes, 2% cashback upto 1000 per quarter on every PolygonGo POS transaction. Except Fuel station transactions.
Note: Terms & Conditions Applied

#### Can a PolygonGo be taken without a card?
PolygonGo (Credit) can only be taken by a VISA Credit Card holder. PolygonGo(Debit) can be taken by a Current/Savings account holder, even without a Debit Card.

#### Can new customers apply for a PolygonGo?
Yes. New customers can apply for a PolygonGo along with their application for a new VISA Credit Card or Current/Savings account

#### Which Current accounts can be used to take a PolygonGo?
Individual or Proprietorship current accounts can be used to take a PolygonGo.

#### Can foreign currency transactions be made with the PolygonGo?
Yes. The PolygonGo chip needs to be endorsed before making transactions in USD or USD equivalent foreign currency.
Note: International POS transactions above BDT 5,000 or USD equivalent cannot be made with the PolygonGo chip, without PIN confirmation.

#### What are the allowable modes of usage?
POS, Ecom and Cash By Code.

#### What is the validity of the PolygonGo chip?
The PolygonGo chip will have a validity of 5 years. Upon expiry of the chip after 5 years, it will be auto renewed with a re-issue fee. A new set of the product (including Wristband and Strap loop) will be sent to the customer.

#### Is there any annual fee for the PolygonGo?
There is no annual fee. Customers will be charged with a one-time issuance fee.

#### What to do in case the PolygonGo chip is stolen/lost?
In case of stolen/lost chip, please immediately deactivate the chip through PolygonTouch or by calling [Polygon Bank hotline]. After deactivating the chip, you can request for a replacement through our call center ([Polygon Bank hotline]), terms and conditions applied.

#### What is the replacement process?
If the chip is stolen/lost/damaged/nonfunctional, it can be replaced by a new set of the product (including Wristband and Strap loop) with a replacement fee. Please call [Polygon Bank hotline] for replacement request.

#### What are the fees and charges for the PolygonGo chip?
The following fees and charges shall be applicable:

| Fee/Charge Type | PolygonGo (Credit) | PolygonGo (Debit) |
|---|---|---|
| Issuance/Re-Issue/Replacement Fee | BDT 3,000 | BDT 3,000 |
| Monthly Interest For Purchase1 | 1.67% | N/A |
| EMI Interest Rate Monthly | 0.92% | N/A |
| Currency Conversion Markup Fee (Other than USD)2 | 3% | 3% |
| Monthly Balance transfer interest rate | 0.92% | N/A |
| Minimum due for monthly payment | BDT 500 or 3% whichever is higher | N/A |
| EMI Early Settlement Fee | 0.5 % of the remaining balance | N/A |
| PIN Regeneration Fee (Call Center Only) | BDT 500 | N/A |
| Fee for Credit Card Payment throughMFS Wallets & Internet Banking Applications3 | BDT 35 | N/A |
| Incoming Fund Transfer3 | N/A | BDT 15 |
| Processing fee for fund transfer from PolygonTouch to CASA and MFS Wallet | BDT 100 or 2% whichever is higher | N/A |
| Fee for Fund Loading to MFS Wallet (Add Money) | 1% | Free |
| ATM cash withdrawal Fee (Cash By Code, for Debit Card Only) | N/A | Free |
| ATM Cash Withdrawal Limit | N/A | As per existing Cash By Code Limit via PolygonTouch |

 

Note

15% VAT shall be charged as applicable.
Compound interest shall be charged on all applicable interest.
Flat interest rate shall be used to calculate the interest payable for EMIs.
Maximum interest free period shall be 45 days and minimum shall be 15 days. Interest shall not be charged if you have paid your previous month’s outstanding amount in due time. If you pay partial amount or minimum amount, the interest on your retail transactions and fees shall be calculated from the following day of your last payment due date.
Interest charges are imposed both on the OUTSTANDING RETAIL TRANSACTION BALANCE & FEES that is not paid within the PAYMENT DUE DATE. Interest shall be calculated from the following day of your last payment due date until full payment.

3% additional markup shall be added at the time of settlement of any international transaction except in USD and shall be reflected on the monthly billing statement.
Note: For PolygonGo (Credit) 3% markup fee is inclusive of VAT.

For credit card bill payment using VISA Direct payment gateway or for incoming fund transfer from all MFS wallets/Internet Banking Applications using VISA Direct payment gateway. Charges will be applicable as per existing process.

#### How will the PolygonGo be delivered?
Debit Card PolygonGo taken for a Current/Savings account will be delivered to customer's preferred branch. Customer can then collect it from the branch. Credit Card PolygonGo taken for a VISA Credit Card will be delivered to customer's communication address.

#### How to permanently close a PolygonGo?
A PolygonGo can be permanently closed by visiting your nearest Polygon Bank Plc branch.

#### Can a PolygonGo be used if the VISA card is closed?
PolygonGo taken for your Current/Savings account can be used even if the VISA Debit Card is closed, provided the account is operational. However, PolygonGo taken for your VISA Credit Card cannot be used if the Credit Card is closed.

Source: https://www.citybankplc.com/p-ca/city-go

Double Benefit Insurance (DBI): Double Benefit Insurance (DBI) insures your outstanding balance during unfortunate events of death or permanent total disability (PTD) by paying off your full outstanding balance and an equal amount to your nominee. The maximum payout amount will not exceed BDT 2,000,000 or twice the outstanding amount, whichever is lower. Once you get enrolled in the program, you will be charged a premium of 0.40% on your monthly card outstanding balance.

### Public Sector Finance

At Polygon Bank, we believe that sustainable national development requires a collaborative partnership between the private and public sectors. With over four decades of experience as a pioneer in Bangladesh's banking sector, we provide specialized financial solutions to government-owned entities, …
#### Our Core Offerings
Infrastructure & Project Finance
Transaction Banking & Trade Services

#### Our Core Offerings
Our dedicated Public Sector team, highly oriented with Government's commercial borrowing policies and guidelines, combined with deep local market knowledge and international banking standards, offers a variety of financial services applicable for Government, Government-owned entities and corporations, including but not limited to:

Infrastructure & Project Finance: We fund capital expenditure for critical national projects, including power and energy, ports, transport, aviation, and digital connectivity. 
Structured Finance & Export Credit Agency (ECA)-backed Finance: We deliver tailored debt solutions and multiple capital market products for major infrastructure in Bangladesh, leveraging ECA-backed facilities and OBU. Our team has successfully raised over USD 2+ billion for corporate clients in Bangladesh.
Transaction Banking & Trade Services: We provide automated cash management, payroll disbursement, and trade solutions for large utilities and regulatory bodies. Our digital platforms - PolygonTouch, PolygonCorp, and Polygon IMPEX - create secure, paperless ecosystems for complex public sector transactions.
Sustainable Finance & Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) Advisory: As the top-ranked bank for sustainability in Bangladesh for five consecutive years, we offer financing and advisory services that align your projects with national and global ESG standards.

#### A Proven Track Record of Partnership
Polygon Bank has established itself as a trusted intermediary between global development finance institutions and domestic infrastructure needs. Our ability to mobilize international capital — demonstrated by landmark financing partnerships with organizations like the Asian Infrastructure Investment Bank (AIIB) and the New Development Bank (NDB) — enables us to support large-scale, long-term national projects.

#### Why Choose Polygon Bank?
Proven Institutional Credibility: Since 1983, we have served as a cornerstone of Bangladesh’s economic growth. Our stability is anchored by shareholding from the International Finance Corporation (IFC) of the World Bank Group, ensuring our operations align with the highest global standards of governance and banking practice.
Global Syndication & Multilateral Partnerships: We act as a primary bridge between local projects and global capital. Our long-standing partnerships with multilateral institutions like the AIIB and NDB, alongside Export Credit Agencies, allow us to structure and secure the large-scale, long-tenure financing required for nation-building infrastructure.
Integrated Digital & Financial Ecosystem: Beyond capital, we provide a sophisticated digital backbone - including PolygonCorp and Polygon IMPEX - that ensures your project’s financial lifecycle is managed through a secure, paperless, and highly efficient ecosystem.

#### Our Core Capabilities
We move beyond traditional lending to offer high-alpha financial structures designed to enhance shareholder wealth and market competitiveness:

Debt Capital Markets (DCM): We leverage deep liquidity pools to match investor appetites with the funding needs of domestic and international issuers.
Risk Management & Derivatives: Utilizing Credit Derivatives, Convertible Bonds, and structured asset solutions to manage risk exposure and achieve target portfolio yields.

### Supply Chain Finance

Polygon Bank's SCF platform is a sophisticated, technology-driven ecosystem designed to optimize working capital for both Anchors Corporate Buyers and their Suppliers. By linking buyers, sellers, and the bank, we reduce liquidity risk and improve business efficiency across your entire value chain.
#### Our Financing Solutions
Factoring — Instant Liquidity against Receivables
Work Order Financing — Bridging the Procurement …
Reverse Factoring — Buyer-Led Supplier Credit
Tailored liquidity to ensure an uninterrupted production cycle.

#### Factoring Instant Liquidity against Receivables
Suppliers can assign their outstanding invoices to the bank to receive immediate funding. This converts credit sales into cash, eliminating the wait for payment terms.

#### Work Order Financing Bridging the Procurement Gap
Get up to 85% of your invoice value funded upon delivery of goods. This facility ensures suppliers have the capital to fulfill orders, with the buyer settling the dues on the maturity date.

#### Reverse Factoring Buyer-Led Supplier Credit
Leverage the credit strength of the Corporate Buyer to provide suppliers with early payment. This strengthens supplier relationships and secures your production line.

#### Ecosystem Benefits
For the Anchor (Buyer)

	

For the Supplier (Seller)

Extended payment terms without straining suppliers.

	

Access to low-cost funding based on the buyer's credit.

Improved supply chain stability and loyalty.

	

Faster conversion of receivables into cash.

Strengthened balance sheet and cash forecasting.

	

Elimination of the need for hard collateral.

#### Why Choose Polygon Bank?
Seamless, Automated Operations: Experience a fully paperless procurement and payment cycle with real-time tracking of limits, invoices, and disbursements.
Optimized Financial Efficiency: Benefit from competitive, risk-adjusted pricing and rapid turnaround times designed to meet your most urgent procurement deadlines.
Flexible & Simplified Access: Enjoy a streamlined onboarding process with minimal documentation, including unique flexibility for small-ticket LCs and niche SME requirements.

### Working Capital Finance

Empowering your business mobility. Optimize your working capital and fortify your global trade footprint with our suite of flexible financing solutions.

#### Working Capital Solutions
Overdraft (OD): Maximize your liquidity with a revolving credit line designed for day-to-day operational agility. Enjoy the flexibility of interest-only payments on utilized balances tailored to your asset profile.
Short-Term Loan (STL): Bridge funding gaps with precision. Our STLs offer competitive market-linked rates and customizable repayment frameworks—including bullet or structured schedules—to align perfectly with your projected cash inflows.

#### Trade and Ecosystem Finance
Trade Finance: Navigate the complexities of the global supply chain with ease. From Pre-shipment financing to Post-shipment liquidity, we provide end-to-end support to accelerate your import/export trade cycles and mitigate counterparty risk.
Bank Guarantees: Enhance your commercial credibility. We issue robust, globally recognized instruments including Bid Bonds, Performance Bonds, and Advance Payment Guarantees to ensure your contractual obligations are backed by our financial strength.

#### Why Choose Polygon Bank?
Reliable Liquidity at All Times: We leverage our long-standing partnership with the International Finance Corporation (IFC) - our largest institutional shareholder - to provide consistent, flexible foreign currency and local currency liquidity. This ensures you have stable access to working capital to fund your operations, regardless of market volatility.
Integrated Digital Trade Ecosystem: We accelerate your cash cycle through our dedicated digital platforms, PolygonCorp and Polygon IMPEX. These tools enable your team to initiate, track, and manage both local and cross-border trade applications and transactions 24/7, replacing paper-heavy processes with streamlined, straight-through processing.
Strong Governance & Regulatory Compliance: We operate under a robust, audited governance framework that ensures all financing activities - including direct import/export bill discounting and transfers to local recipients - fully comply with international banking standards and Bangladesh Bank requirements. Your operations are backed by institutional stability and rigorous risk management.

### Additional Benefits For Rider

Anyones life may be affected by a sudden sickness or accident at any time such unexpected incidents could cause financial loss that may affect other plans to implement. Hospitalization due to sickness or account is usually stressful in this worse time, hospitalization expense makes matters worse. …

#### Benefits
ADB- Accidental Death Benefit

If the insured dies due to an external accident, in addition to the sum insured payable on the natural death of the insured, an amount equal to the rider amount is paid as per the contract. The maximum risk amount of this ADB is 20 lakh taka and the premium rate is 1.25 taka per thousand BDT.

 

PDAB-Permanent Disability & Accidental Benefit

This supplementary insurance provides financial assistance to the insured in case of permanent disability due to accident. If the insured becomes unable to carry out any profession or occupation due to permanent physical disability, a 'supplementary' benefit is provided at the rate of 10% of the main insurance amount every year for a maximum of 10 years.

In case of permanent complete disability, the premium is waived. In case of permanent partial/complete disability of any part of the body, compensation is given as per the schedule. However, compensation is not given for any disability that has already occurred. The premium of this supplementary insurance is only 3.50 taka for every thousand taka of the sum insured. The maximum sum insured of this supplementary insurance is 20 lakh taka and the age at maturity will be 60 years.

 

HI-Hospital Insurance

Under this supplementary insurance scheme, a maximum of 20 percent of the basic sum insured is paid for actual expenses incurred during hospitalization. The maximum amount of this claim is 5 lakh taka. If the insured wishes, his wife or husband and children can take advantage of this insurance simultaneously.

 

MDB 25%- Major Decease Benefit 25%

The aim of this scheme is to reduce the financial and mental burden of any serious illness. If the insured is affected by any of the 7 specified serious illnesses, 25% of the sum assured is paid immediately. The remaining 75% is paid at the time of claim settlement as usual. The maximum sum assured under this scheme is 20 lakh taka. The illnesses for which the sum assured is paid partially – Heart attack, Stroke, Coronary artery surgery, Cancer, Kidney failure, Multiple Sclerosis and Paralysis. The maximum age limit is 60 years.

 

MDB 50%- Major Decease Benefit 50%

The maximum sum insured in this plan is 10 lakh taka. The maximum age limit is 60 years. 8 diseases.  Heart attack, Stroke, Coronary artery surgery, Cancer, Kidney failure, Multiple Sclerosis, Paralysis and Major organ transplantation.

 

FIR- Family Income Rider

If the insured dies during the insurance period, 25% of the principal sum assured is paid immediately along with the bonus earned till the date of death. The remaining 75% is paid at the end of the term. In addition, 10% of the principal sum assured is paid to the family as an allowance on a monthly basis till the maturity. The term can be 10, 15 or 20 years. The maximum age limit is 60 years.

Source: https://www.citybankplc.com/p/additional-benefits-for-rider

#### Policy Benefit
At Maturity: On maturity full sum assured with accrued bonus will be paid to the Policy Holder. 
At Death: In case of death of the Policy Holder & the policy is active, the full Sum Assured along with accrued bonus will be paid to the nominee(s).
Income Tax Rebate facility:
The policy Holder will get benefit of 15% income tax rebate on the premium paid every year as per the income tax policy of the government.
Death and living claim amount is completely tax free.
Paid up Value: After continuing the policy at least two years, the policy acquires Paid up value. Paid up value is payable with accrued bonus at the end of policy term.
Automatic Policy Loan (APL): If the Policy Holder misses a premium payment, the APL features automatically laps into policy holder’s policy’s cash value to cover the premium preventing policy from lapsing (T&C applicable).
Surrender Value: The Policy Holder can apply for the surrender value of the insurance policy at any time after paying at least 2 years of premium.
Karje Hasana: Upto 90% of the net cash surrender/surrender value can be taken as Karje Hasana (Interest-free Insurance Loan).

Monthly Tk 1% of Sum Assured will be provided to the child as stipend till maturity of the plan.
All future Premiums will be waived.
Full sum assured including accrued bonus will be paid to designated legal guardian at maturity.

In case of Permanent & Total Disability:

All future Premiums will be waived

In case of the death of the beneficiary child:

During the term of the policy if the child dies then policy holder can nominate another child.
If there’s no child, the policy will automatically be converted to ordinary endowment policy.
When there is Education Stipend in payment, the recipient child dies and there is no one else to be nominated, the company can pay discounted value of the remaining stipend.

 

Income Tax Rebate facility:
The policy Holder will get benefit of 15% income tax rebate on the premium paid every year as per the income tax policy of the government.
Death and living claim amount is completely tax free.
Paid up Value: After continuing the policy at least two years, the policy acquires Paid up value. Paid up value is payable with accrued bonus at the end of policy term.
Automatic Policy Loan (APL): If the Policy Holder misses a premium payment, the APL features automatically laps into policy holder’s policy’s cash value to cover the premium preventing policy from lapsing (T&C applicable).
Surrender Value: The Policy Holder can apply for the surrender value of the insurance policy at any time after paying at least 2 years of premium.
Karje Hasana: Upto 90% of the net cash surrender/surrender value can be taken as Karje Hasana (Interest-free Insurance Loan).

#### User guides & tutorials

Browse common questions and quick answers—all organized for easy navigation.

#### How can I register for PolygonTouch?
You can register from the PolygonTouch app by tapping Start Using PolygonTouch > Register Now and entering your Polygon Bank account number or card number. You will need to input your registered mobile number or email, create a User ID, set a password and complete the required security setup.

#### What is TPIN and how can I reset it if I forget it?
TPIN is a 6-digit Transaction PIN used to authorize financial transactions in PolygonTouch. If you forget your TPIN or it gets locked, you can reset it from Profile > TPIN Settings > Reset TPIN. Polygon Bank, branch or call center cannot recover or share your existing TPIN, as it is not stored in a readable format.

#### How can I open an account through PolygonTouch?
You can open an individual bank account digitally through PolygonTouch by selecting Create an Account from the app. You will need your NID, mobile number, address information and live selfie verification to complete the account opening journey.

#### How can I raise or track a transaction dispute in PolygonTouch?
You can raise a dispute from Services > Transaction History by selecting the transaction and tapping Raise Dispute. You can also raise a dispute from the transaction receipt page. To track a submitted dispute, go to Services > My Dispute or Profile > Report Issues > Check Updates.

#### How can I pay bills through PolygonTouch?
Go to Pay & Transfer and select the bill category, such as electricity, gas, water, internet, cable TV, education, insurance or credit card bill payment. Enter the required bill information, select the source account or card and complete the transaction.

#### How can I download statements, certificates or receipts from PolygonTouch?
You can download account statements, card statements, tax certificates, solvency certificates, loan certificates from the relevant Services menu and transaction receipts from Transaction History feature.

#### How can I manage my cards from PolygonTouch?
You can manage card-related services from Services > Card Services or from the card detail page. Available options include Card PIN Reset, International Transaction Management, Virtual Card Application, EMI Conversion and other card services.

#### How can customers update their mobile number or email address in PolygonTouch?
Customers can update their contact details from Services > PolygonTouch Services by selecting Update Mobile Number or Update Email Address and completing the required verification steps.

Source: https://www.citybankplc.com/ct/citytouch
