Unmatched Accessibility

#### Unmatched Accessibility: Anytime, Anywhere
Break free from banking hours. Polygon IMPEX provides 24/7 accessibility, allowing you to manage your trade business from any location, on any device. Whether you are at the office, home, or traveling, your trade transactions are always within reach.

#### Easing Import Trade
Streamline your procurement with digital precision.

Easy LC Application: Initiate Letter of Credit requests with intuitive, user-friendly templates.
Real-time Limit Checking: Monitor your available trade limits instantly before applying.
Transaction Updates: Get live status tracking on all trade cycle milestones.
Comprehensive History: Access your complete transaction archives and download trade advices at the click of a button.

#### Smart Value Additions
Go beyond basic banking with advanced management tools.

Draft LC Generation: Create and share draft LCs for beneficiary approval before final submission to the bank.

Advanced Analytics: Access specialized MIS reports to gain deep insights into your trade volume, and outstanding liabilities.

#### Easing Export Operations
Empower your export business with dedicated automation.

Group Module: Centralized management for multiple export contracts and LCs.
System-Driven Ceiling Management: Automated tracking of export limits to ensure you never miss an opportunity.

Digital Loan Requests: Apply for Pre-shipment and Post-shipment export financing directly through the portal.

#### Fees and charges
This page does not list fees for this product; the bank's schedule of charges applies.

Source: https://www.citybankplc.com/p/city-impex

### G Pay

Google Pay
For the first time in Bangladesh, Polygon Bank, in collaboration with Google, Mastercard and Visa, introduces Google Wallet, a smart and seamless digital payment solution. Starting today, customers can add their Polygon Bank issued Mastercard or Visa cards to Google Wallet to make contactless payments …
Tap to pay-fast and easy
PIN-free transactions for small purchases
Secured by Googles advanced encryption technology
Works worldwide at any NFC-enabled POS terminal
Take a look at the most commonly asked questions. We are here to help

#### Know More About Google Pay?
Q. what’s the relationship between Wallet and Pay? Are they separate? 

Answer: There is no separate app called "Google Pay” available in Bangladesh. The cards stored in Google Wallet are used to make payments via Google Pay. In this way, Wallet and Pay complement each other — one stores your cards, and the other facilitates payment using those cards.

Q. Which Polygon Bank cards are supported on Google Wallet? 

Answer: Polygon Bank All Visa and MasterCard (Debit, Credit and Prepaid cards) can be added to Google Wallet.

Q: What features will be available on Google Wallet? 

Answer: Currently, only payment services at POS machines that are supported NFC wallet transactions.

Q. How can I add my Polygon Bank card to Google Wallet? 

Answer: Steps to add:

Open the Google Wallet app on your Android device.
Tap on “Add to Wallet” → “Payment card”.
Use the camera to scan your card or enter card details manually.
Follow the on-screen authentication process (OTP will be sent to your registered mobile number).
Once verified, the card will be added and ready to use.

Do not share OTP with any one. 

Q. What devices are compatible with Google Wallet? 

Answer: Any Android smartphone running Android 7.0 or later, with NFC capability.

Q. Do I need to internet connection to add card in Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes, Internet connection is required to add card in Google Wallet.

Q. Where can I use Google Wallet? 

Answer: You can use Google Wallet at POS machine available at merchant location (locally or internationally) that support NFC wallet transaction.

Q. How do I pay using Google Wallet in stores? 

 Answer:

Unlock your phone.
Hold it near the contactless payment terminal.
Wait for the confirmation checkmark or vibration, and you’re done!

You can tap to pay anywhere you see the contactless or Google Pay symbols and POS machine that support NFC wallet transaction

Q. Can I add multiple Polygon Bank MasterCard and Visa Cards to Google Wallet? 

Answer:  Yes, you can add multiple Polygon Bank MasterCard and Visa credit or debit cards to Google Wallet. Simply follow the “Add to Wallet” steps for each card. You can choose a default card anytime.

Note: All contactless payments will be made from the card set as the default.

Q: How can I set or remove a default card in Google Wallet? 

Answer: The default card is the one used automatically for contactless payments (tap-to-pay) at NFC wallet transaction enabled POS machines.

To set a default card in Google Wallet:

Open the Google Wallet app.
Tap on the card you want to set as default.
Tap "Details" or the three-dot menu (â‹®).
Select "Set as default for tap to pay."

To remove a default card:

You cannot remove a default card without first assigning another card as default. To do this:

Add another card to your wallet (if not already added).
Set the new card as default using the steps above.
Then tap on the previous card > tap the three-dot menu (â‹®) > select "Remove payment method."

Q. Can I add my Polygon Bank MasterCard or Visa Cards to multiple Google Wallet? 

Answer:  Yes, you can your MasterCard and Visa cards in multiple to Google Wallet.

Q. Is it secure to use my card through Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes. Google Wallet uses tokenization, meaning your actual card number is never shared with the merchant. Additionally, your phone’s security features (e.g., screen lock or biometrics) help ensure only you can authorize purchases.

Q. Do I need to unlock my phone to pay? 

Answer: Yes, for security reasons, the phone must be unlocked using a screen lock (PIN, fingerprint, or pattern) before payment can be made.

Q. Do I need to internet connection to pay using google pay? 

Answer: No, Internet connection is not required to pay at Store.

Q. Will I need to enter a PIN for transactions? 

Answer: As per Bangladesh Bank regulations, NFC transactions above BDT 5,000 will require the PIN to be entered at the POS terminal.

Q. Can I do transactions at QR through Google Wallet? 

Answer: No — you cannot use Google Wallet (or Google Pay) to make QR-code payments in Bangladesh

Q. Can I use Google Wallet for online or in-app purchases? 

Answer: Currently, Polygon Bank Visa and Mastercard cards added to Google Wallet can be used for in-store (POS) contactless payments only. Online and in-app usage support will be communicated if enabled in future.

Q. What should I do if I lose my phone? 

Answer: If your phone is lost or stolen:

Call Polygon Bank’s 24/7 Contact Center at [Polygon Bank hotline] or [Polygon Bank hotline] (from abroad) to block your card.
Use Find My Device (https://www.google.com/android/find) to remotely lock or erase your device.

Q. Will I still earn rewards/benefits if I pay via Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes, all rewards/cashback/benefits (if any) associated with your Polygon Bank Visa or Mastercard will remain unchanged when you pay using Google Wallet.

Q.  What if I face an issue adding my card or during a transaction? 

Answer: Please contact Polygon Bank’s 24/7 Contact Center at:
[Polygon Bank hotline] (within Bangladesh)
[Polygon Bank hotline] (from abroad)

Q. Is there any charge for using Google Wallet? 

Answer: No. Polygon Bank does not charge any fee for enabling or using your card through Google Wallet. Standard card fees and interest (if applicable) remain unchanged.

Q. Can I remove my card from Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes. Open the Google Wallet app → Select your card → Tap the three-dot menu → Choose Remove.

Q. How do I remove my old card from Google Wallet if I’ve replaced or renewed it? 

Answer: 

Open the Google Wallet app.
Select the old/expired card.
Tap the three-dot menu (or “More” option) and choose Remove Payment Method (or a similar option).
Add your new card by tapping Add to Wallet and following the prompts.

Q. What if my Polygon Bank Card is lost, stolen, or damaged and I receive a replacement card? 

Answer: 

Lost or Stolen Card: Once you report your card lost/stolen and receive a new Polygon Bank card, you must add this new card to Google Wallet. The old card is no longer valid, so you should remove it from your Google Wallet app.
Damaged Card: If you requested a replacement due to damage, follow the same steps—remove the old card from Google Wallet and add the new card once you receive it.

Q: What should I do if I find my card or mobile Device has been lost / stolen or feel my card or mobile Device data has been compromised? 

Answer:  Call [Polygon Bank hotline] immediately to notify about the lost/stolen card incident. Following your notification, the bank will restrict your card from any unauthorized transaction. However, the bank shall not take responsibility of an unauthorized transaction if it has happened prior your notification.

Q: What should I do if find a transaction SMS alert/call that has not been made by me? 

Answer:: Call us immediately at [Polygon Bank hotline] and lodge a complaint regarding this.

Q: Whom should I contact for any sort of transaction dispute issue? 

Answer:: Call at [Polygon Bank hotline] for any sort of cards dispute related queries

Q: My phone is Samsung – what NFC setting should I use to make Google Pay work? 

Answer:: For Samsung mobile users, please ensure the following settings for Google Pay to work properly:

Set Google Wallet as the default NFC payment app. Go to Settings → Connections → NFC and contactless payments → Select Google Wallet as the default payment application. 

Q: I can’t add my card to Google Wallet. What should I do? 

Answer: Some users may be unable to add their cards due to a device risk score issue, which means the device doesn’t meet Google’s security requirements. Please ensure the following:

Your phone is running Android 7.0 or higher. The device is Play Protect certified (not rooted or running a custom ROM). A screen lock (PIN, Pattern, or Fingerprint) is enabled. You are using an official version of Google Play Services. If your device still isn’t eligible, you may try adding the card using another compatible device. 

Q: I’ve added my card, but Tap and Pay isn’t working. What should I check? 

Answer: Please ensure the following if Tap and Pay is not functioning:

NFC is turned on in your phone’s settings. Google Wallet is set as the default Tap & Pay app. Your card is active and not expired. You are tapping on a contactless-enabled POS terminal. Your phone screen is on and unlocked at the time of payment. 

Q: Can I avail the Buy 1 Get 1 Free buffet offer when paying with Google Pay? 

Answer: No, currently the Buy 1 Get 1 Free buffet offer is not applicable for payments made through Google Pay. To enjoy this offer, please use your eligible physical Polygon Bank credit card directly at the participating hotel or restaurant.

#### What is Google Wallet?
- Google Wallet is a digital storage solution where user can securely store your bank cards, transit passes, and more. Its primary function is to store data — not to make payments directly.

#### What is Google Pay?
- Google Pay is a payment service that allows user to complete transactions using Google’s ecosystem. It works across various Google services such as the Play Store, Wallet, and more.

#### Fees and charges
This page does not list fees for this product; the bank's schedule of charges applies.

Source: https://www.citybankplc.com/p/g-pay

### Investment Window

Government Securities Investment Window
Polygon Bank provides the following services Under Government Securities Investment Window for the its clients:
Providing advice on Investment in treasury bill and bond
Making arrangement to open securities account (Sub-BP ID with Bangladesh Bank)
Executing buy/sell orders as per client requisition
Completing transactions related to transferring coupon, principal and/or other proceeds in connection with buying and selling securities

Eligibility
1. What is a Government Security? A Government Security is a tradable instrument issued by sovereign Government. It acknowledges the Government's debt obligation. Such securities are short term (usually called treasury bills, with original maturities of less than one year) or long term (usually called Government bonds or dated securities with original maturity of one year or more). 2. What are the major Government Securities (G-Sec) in Bangladesh? Major G-Secs in Bangladesh are: a. Treasury Bills (T-Bills) b. Bangladesh Government Treasury Bond (BGTB) 3. Who issues T-Bills and BGTBs? As per agreement between The Government of Bangladesh and Bangladesh Bank in 1985 (Treasury rules-1998 (Appendix-1, Section-3) and Bangladesh Bank (BB) Order-1972, article 20 empowers BB to issue new loans and manage public debt for the Government. 4. What are the main features of Treasury Bills? Three tenors are available namely 91 day, 182 day and 364 day. Price is determined by the market. They are issued at a discount and redeemed at the face value at maturity. Tradable in the secondary market. Issued in script less form. The central Bank releases monthly calendar through BB website. 5. What are the main features of Treasury Bonds? Risk free fixed coupon bearing debt instrument. Maturities are available within 2-20 years. It carries half yearly coupon payment and the principal is repaid on maturity. Yield is determined by the market. Tradable instrument in the secondary market. Issued in script less form. 6. How can an investor invest in Government securities through The Polygon Bank Ltd? An interested investor needs a Sub-BP (Sub –Business Partner) ID to invest. After opening Sub-BP ID, investors can participate in auctions and trade in the secondary market through The Polygon Bank Ltd. The bank also transfers coupon and principal amounts from Bangladesh Bank to the investor’s account. 7. How T-Bills and BGTBs are issued? Weekly (usually on Sunday) auctions of Treasury Bills are held following a pre-announced calendar with specified amounts. Bidders quote their prices, and the Auction Committee determines the cut-off price. Weekly (usually on Tuesday) auction of BGTB of a particular tenor is held. New issue bidders quote expected yields, while re-issue bidders quote price. 8. What Benefits Can you derive by Investing Treasury Bonds? Absolutely risk free as issued by the sovereign government. Attractive interest rate since yield is market determined. Tradable in the secondary market, providing instant liquidity. All receipts of interest and maturity are fully repatriable for foreign investors. Central Bank provides fully automated scriptless depository system (Market Infrastructure Module). 9. Who can place bid in the auction? Primary Dealers (PDs), other commercial banks, non-bank financial institutions, insurance companies, corporates, and provident fund authorities can participate through PDs. 10. What is the minimum bid amount in an auction? Minimum bid amount is Taka one lac and its multiples. 11. Who can invest in G-Sec? Banks, non-bank financial institutions Insurance companies Corporate bodies Authorities responsible for provident/pension funds 12. Can any individual investor buy G-Secs? Yes, through Primary Dealers/Scheduled Banks in auctions or in the secondary market. 13. How can any individual buy G-Secs? Scheduled Banks or financial institutions open Business Partner Identification (BP ID) in Market Infrastructure (MI) Module of Bangladesh Bank. Individuals can also buy/sell G-Secs OTC or via Trader Work Station (TWS) of MI Module. 14. Can foreigners/Non-residents buy G-Secs? Yes, they can buy BGTB through Primary Dealers or Banks. Payments are made from Non-resident Foreign Currency or NITA accounts. Coupons and resale proceeds are freely transferable after taxes. Bonds can be resold anytime. 15. What type of Bonds can foreign investors purchase? Eligible foreign investors can purchase, sell, or transfer Treasury Bonds of any maturity. 16. What is the minimum bid amount in an auction? Minimum bid amount is Taka one lac (0.1 million) and multiples. 17. Is premature encashment available for G-Secs? No, but holders can sell G-Secs OTC or in the secondary market. 18. Is it possible to borrow against Govt securities? Banks and financial institutions can use government securities as collateral only to borrow funds in the repo market.

### Polygon Foreign Exchange

The city Bank's Foreign Remittance unit meets growing customer needs for fast, sucure & easy money transfers to an extensive range of destinations. Being a committed bank to its customers, we go all the lengths to remit your hard earned money safely to your loved ones. With us, apart from a range …

#### Facility
Polygon Bank Limited has 132 online branches across the country, Besides, the Bank has a strong remittance network with other major banks of the country. Therefore, wherever your account is, we are able to send your money instantly.

If you are a Polygon Bank account holder, then please visit any of our branches. Our Foreign remittance service personnel will be there to help you out. If you are not an account holder, then please open an account of your choice with us to receive your remittance at earliest convenience.

CBL understands the value of your precious time. That's why have made the payment procedure simple & easy. You have the privilege of enchashing the remitted money instantly from your branch counter without going through any hassle. That is to say, if you are an account holder of Polygon Bank, we can instantly credit the money to your account or pay cash to the receiver.

So, place your trust with Polygon Foreign Remittance Service. Send your money to your loved ones & experience peace of mind

### Agent Banking

With a view to spread banking facilities to the root level throughout the country, Polygon Bank brings agent banking. Now enjoy our banking facilities at your reach.
Easy & Secure
ways of money transfer
Quick remittance
money pick up
Pay utility bills
skipping the queue

#### Mission and objectives
Simplifying all banking needs of the customers and reaching them out to customers' doorsteps are the mission and objective of Polygon Agent Banking.

#### Services
Opening savings, current, pay scale, student and other accounts
Cash deposit and withdrawal
Transferring funds to other banks or accounts without any hassle
Providing remittance money
Checking balance
Providing mini statement
Taking bills (electricity bill, gas bill, mobile bill etc)
Taking clearing cheque
Taking applications for micro and agriculture loans
DPS/fixed deposit services
Taking school and college fees

#### Eligibility
Nationality has to be Bangladeshi
Age should be above 18 years (except for student account)
NID/passport/driving license/local chairman's certificate with attested photo/birth certificate and any photo-ID
2 copies passport size photographs of customer
One copy photo and photo-ID of the nominee
Customer must have a trade license to open a business account

If a customer is eligible for opening account, agent will provide the account opening form.

#### Facilities
Cash deposit
Fund transfer
Utility bill payment
Taking remittance
Cash withdrawal

Other Facilities:

Payment of salary in area based business institute through the outlet
Taking clearing cheque of any other bank in agent outlet
Collecting application for micro and agriculture loan
Payment of school and college fees through agent outlet
Providing all types of card related information through agent outlet
Receiving application to activate internet banking
Receiving insurance premium
Receiving monthly installment of association/society

#### Outlets
Click here to view the list of active Agent Banking outlets, and click here to view the list of closed Agent Banking outlets.

#### Eligibility
Offshore Banking (OBU) Account 

Person resident outside Bangladesh including NRBs
Foreign nationals
Persons of Bangladeshi origin (Bangladeshi holding foreign citizenship)
Companies/Firms registered and operating abroad
Foreign institutional investors
Type A units in EPZs (Export Processing Zones, Private EPZs, Economic Zones and Hi-tech parks) in Bangladesh

Note: All individuals will have to be of age 18 & above.

International Banking (IB) Account

The following individuals or entities having bonafide relations with non-residents can open/operate International Banking (IB) Accounts:
 

Any Resident Bangladeshi individual (aged 18 years & above)
Bangladeshi Corporates/Firms
Type A, Type B, Type C industrial enterprises operating in EPZs (Export Processing Zones)/EZs (Economic Zones)/HTPs (High Tech Parks)

#### Required Documents

Individual Account

Copy of valid photo ID of applicant, i.e., passport, respective foreign country’s ID, etc.
If the photo ID is a Bangladeshi passport, additional documents such as resident visa, foreign resident card (i.e., green card), work permit, driving license (issued by foreign authority) or any other document supporting the residency status abroad is required.
Passport sized photograph: applicant – 2 copies, and nominee 1 copy attested by applicant.
Address verification document required only if the declared address is different from the address in the applicant’s Photo ID document. This can be utility bill, driving license (issued by foreign authority) etc.

Non-Individual Account

Extract of Resolution/Board Resolution
Trade License or similar document
Certificate of Incorporation
Memorandum and Articles of Association
Updated Schedule X & Form XII or any recent document that proves shareholding information and names of the Board of Directors with respective designation.
Photo identification of shareholders (Top 5 shareholders’ details required when shareholders are more than 5. If the shareholders are less than 5, then all shareholders’/ all authorized signatories’ for the account/ the beneficial owners’ of the Company)
2 copy passport size photo of each shareholder (Top 5 shareholders’ details required when shareholders are more than 5. If the shareholders are less than 5, then all shareholders’/ all authorized signatories’ for the account/ the beneficial owners’ of the Company)

 

Individual Account

Copy of Valid Photo ID for account holder/ Applicant and Nominee (Such as Passport / National ID/ Birth Certificate etc.)
Passport size Photograph of the Applicant – 2 Copy, Nominee duly attested by the applicant – 1 Copy.
Address Verification Documents if Address not Matched with Applicant Photo ID e.g. Utility Bill etc.
An Undertaking Form duly signed by the Remitter. This will be required before crediting any foreign currency into the account.

Non-Individual Account

Trade License or alternative document
Certificate of Incorporation
Updated Form X & XII
Photo Identification of Shareholder (Top 5 Shareholder's details when Share Holder is more than 5. If the shareholder is less than 5 then all shareholder’s/All authorized Signatories for the account/The beneficial owners of the Company)
2 copy Passport size Photo of Shareholder (Top 5 Shareholder's details when Share Holder is more than 5. If the shareholder is less than 5 then all shareholder’s/All authorized Signatories for the account/The beneficial owners of the Company) 

#### Frequently Asked Questions OBU Account
1. What is Offshore Banking Account?

Account that can be opened by Residents outside Bangladesh in foreign currency. For example: Non-resident Bangladeshi nationals (NRBs), Persons of Bangladeshi origin (Bangladeshi holding foreign citizenship), Foreign nationals, Companies/Firms registered and operating abroad, Foreign institutional investors etc. can open this OBU account.

2. Who can open OBU Account?

Foreign nationals

3. Can customers open a joint OBU account?

Yes, customers can open a joint OBU account. But in such cases both the account holders must individually qualify to open the account as per question 1 or 2 above.

4. What type of account can a customer open?

A customer can open a Fixed Deposit account in foreign currency (USD or EUR) under this program. To credit the funds for the Fixed Deposit account, the customer has to open a current account first. This current account will help to facilitate fund transfers, remittances and other account operations. So, basically the customer will have to have both Current and Fixed deposit account(s) in OBU.

5. Documents required to open OBU account

Document check list for Individuals

Document check list for OBU account for companies/firms registered and operating abroad.

Certificate of Incorporation
Photo identification of shareholders
(Top 5 shareholders’ details required when shareholders are more than 5. If the shareholders are less than 5, then all shareholders’/ all authorized signatories’ for the account/ the beneficial owners’ of the Company)
2 copy passport size photo of each shareholder

6. Is there a physical OBU account opening form?

For individual OBU account opening no physical form will be required. Customer can open account online through the customer service portal in our website.

If the customer is physically present at branch, they can use the regular account opening form. For institutional accounts, the customer can download the form here.

7. Can an existing Polygon Bank customer open OBU account?

Yes, any existing Polygon Bank customer can open OBU account if he or she qualifies for OBU account as per question 1 or 2 above.

8. In which currency can a customer open OBU account?

Currently at Polygon Bank, a customer can open OBU account in USD & EUR currency only.

9. Is there any minimum deposit threshold required to open the account?

For maintaining a current account, no minimum balance is required. For Fixed deposit account the minimum deposit amount is USD/EUR 5,000.

10. What are the tenors available to open Fixed deposit account?

The available tenors are 3 months, 6 months, 1 year, 2 years, 3 years, 4 years and 5 years.

11. How will the customer use the balance of the current account?

The balance of the account can be used for any of the following purposes:

Transferring to on-shore banking BDT accounts to meet necessary payments and investments.
Making outward remittances of the entire balance as and when necessary.

12. Can a customer open a Fixed deposit account without opening a current account?

To open a Fixed deposit account, opening a current account is a mandatory requirement.

13. Is nominee mandatory for OBU account?

Yes, nominee is required for Individual accounts (both current and Fixed deposit) only.

14. Can a customer add multiple nominees to the OBU account?

Yes, the customer can add multiple nominees to the account. For each nominee, we will need photo identification document and one copy passport sized photograph.

15. Can a customer open OBU account in any branch?

Yes, the customer can physically come to open OBU account in any Polygon Bank branch during his/her visit to Bangladesh.

16. Is there any maximum deposit limit for OBU account?

No, there is no maximum limit for OBU account.

17. Can a customer avail Credit card or Debit card against OBU account?

Since the OBU account is opened for the purpose of Fixed Deposit only, the bank will not issue any debit/credit card against this account. 

18. Can a customer open multiple currency OBU account?

Yes, a customer can open OBU accounts in both USD and EUR as per their requirement.

19. Can a customer open OBU account in different banks?

Yes, a customer is free to open multiple OBU accounts in any bank they desire.

20. Can a customer open OBU account in Islamic module?

No, currently this option is not available with Polygon Bank.

#### Frequently Asked Questions IB Account
Account Opening:

2. Who can open OBU Account?

Foreign nationals

Certificate of Incorporation

 

Account Operations:
 

1. How can the account holder credit funds in his account?

Fund can be credited by the account holder through foreign inward remittances received through official banking channels only.

2. Who can send funds (originator) to the OBU account?

Anyone can send funds to the OBU account from any country. There are no restrictions on who can send funds. But the Fixed Deposit account will be opened in the name of the current account holder (Beneficiary).

For example: The current account is opened in the name Mr. Alam.  Mr. Alam can remit funds from abroad to his account. Also anyone else like Mr. Hasan or Mr. Ahmed both can send funds to the account of Mr. Alam. But the Fixed deposit account will be in the name of Mr. Alam only. 

3. Is there any cheque book facility available against OBU account?

No, there is no cheque book facility available. The account can be operated by sending remittance or transfer instructions to the bank.

4. Is it possible to deposit Dollar/EUR in cash form?

Cash deposit is not possible in OBU account. Funds can only be credited by inward remittances received through official channels.

5. Is it possible to withdraw any amount from the account in cash form?

Cash withdrawal is not possible from OBU account.

6. Are OBU customers allowed to avail PolygonTouch, the internet banking app of Polygon Bank?

Yes, PolygonTouch can be used by Individual account holders for viewing purpose only. No transaction can be done through PolygonTouch. They may use it for sending some instructions to the bank.

7. Is there any limit on the number of transactions to a single OBU account?

No there is no limit on the number of transactions in the account.

8. What is the procedure to transfer the balance from foreign currency OBU account to an onshore local currency (BDT) account?

OBU customers can transfer funds from foreign currency OBU account to any local currency account by applying to the bank through the remittance request form .

09. What is the process for Fixed Deposit Encashment?

You can download the Fixed Deposit Encashment form here and fill out and email the signed form to [Polygon Bank email] from your registered email address.
However, if you have already given instruction for encashment at time of opening the Fixed Deposit in the Fixed Deposit opening Form, the deposit will be encashed as per your instructions.

1. Is there any fees and charges for maintaining OBU account?

There are no fees and charges for maintaining OBU account and there is no hidden fee also. Details of the fees and charges are given below:

Deposit Rate:
 

 

 

1. Is OBU account an interest bearing account?

Yes, the bank is offering attractive interest rates on Fixed Deposit accounts. However, Current account is a non-interest bearing account. Please click here to know the current offers of Fixed Deposit accounts.

2. Is interest rate fixed in OBU Fixed deposit account?

The interest rate is based on the benchmark rate of the respective currencies which may change on daily basis. Based on the benchmark rate of the respective currencies, the interest rate may vary.

For the Fixed Deposit, the interest rate on the date of booking will be applicable till the maturity of the deposit. Our prevailing deposit interest rates are available in our website.

3. What is a “Benchmark Rate”?

Benchmark or reference rate is a rate that reflects the cost of borrowing in a particular currency based on international demand and supply.

4. What are the benchmark rates of the respective currencies?

Currency Code

	

Benchmark

US Dollar

	

Secured Overnight
Financing Rate (SOFR)

Euro

	

Euro Short-term Rate (ESTR)

 

5. How can I find out the interest rate for Fixed deposits?

To know the interest rates of different tenors that are applicable today, please click here.

6. What interest rate will be applied for premature encashment of OBU Fixed deposit?

If the deposit is kept for less than 3 months, no interest will be paid. For premature encashment after 3 months, applicable rate will the interest rate corresponding to the nearest/immediate lower tenor’s prevailing published interest rate or the FD booking interest rate, whichever is lower.
For example, if you open a Fixed Deposit for 3 years at 8% and want to encash/close after 1 year, 1 year’s prevailing fixed deposit rate will be applicable. But if at that time the 1 year interest rate is higher than 8%, then it will encashed at 8%.

Fund Transfer:

1. What information is required to send remittances to any Polygon Bank account from abroad?

To send remittances, please use below information for beneficiary-

SWIFT Code of Polygon Bank                 : CIBLBDDH

Bank Name                                       : Polygon Bank

Customer’s OBU Account Number    : ……………….

Account Name                                  : …

2. Through which banks can remittances be sent to Polygon Bank?

You can send remittance from any bank in the world to the account in Polygon Bank.
When you are sending the remittance, if your bank from where you are sending the fund requests for bank account information of Polygon Bank i.e., the correspondent foreign banks (intermediary bank) of Polygon Bank, you can share the bank details from this link for convenience: here.

3. What will be the conversion rate if a customer wants to convert the foreign currency balance to BDT to an on-shore bank account?

The bank will use the prevailing TT clean Buying rate of the bank on the date of the conversion. The exchange rates are available here

4. Is the balance in OBU account freely transferable abroad?

Yes, the entire balance of OBU account is freely transferable abroad. Please find remittance request form here

5. If I have OBU account in one currency, can I send outward remittance in other currency?

Yes, you can. In such cases the cross currency conversion will take place.

6. Can customers transfer the balance of OBU account to another of his OBU account?

Yes, the customer can transfer the balance of OBU account to another OBU account.

7. Is remittance incentive paid by the government applicable for OBU deposits?

No, remittance incentive is not applicable to OBU deposits or encashment.

8. What is the SWIFT code of Polygon Bank?

Our SWIFT Code is CIBLBDDH. It is also known as BIC (Bank Identification Code).

Tax & Excise Duty:
 

1. Is there any tax or any duty applicable on the deposit or interest?

As per new Offshore Banking Act passed by National Parliament of Bangladesh on 14 March 2024, depositors are exempted from any tax on the balance of OBU account and on any interest paid. Also no excise duty is applicable on the balance of the account.

General & Others:

 

1. How is Polygon Bank offering such high-interest rates on OBU account deposits?

Offshore banking basically is dependent on foreign borrowings @ 2%-3% margin on SOFR/benchmark rates. Polygon Bank aims to expand its long-term customer deposit base by providing competitive deposit rates aligned with regulatory guidelines. Therefore, Polygon Bank is offering these competitive rates instead of borrowing from abroad and it will save cost to the bank as well as reduce market dependency.

2. Is there any possibility to obtain Commercially Important Person (CIP) status from government of Bangladesh by maintaining funds in an OBU account?

Since this is an investment in an OBU account, this is out of the scope of non-resident remittance category which is considered for CIP status by the government.

Subsidiaries & Group Entities
#### Polygon Bank Capital Resources Limited
A leading investment bank in Bangladesh, it is a wholly owned subsidiary of Polygon Bank. Since its establishment in 2010, it has built a strong presence in corporate advisory, equity and debt capital management, and portfolio management.
#### Polygon Brokerage Limited
A wholly-owned subsidiary of Polygon Bank, it is one of the leading brokerages in the capital market of Bangladesh. CBL has been pioneer in introducing a unique business model with emphasis on digital innovation and fin-tech integration of investment ecosystem to cater the needs of retail segment, NRBs, local and foreign institutional investors.
#### CBL Money Transfer Sdn. Bhd. (Malaysia)
A wholly-owned subsidiary of Polygon Bank, it is a licensed Money Service Business Company (MSB) in Malaysia and specialized in cross-border remittance services for migrant workers and expatriates working and living in Malaysia. It facilitates fund transfer through the integrated mobile remittance app PolygonRemit.
#### Polygon Hong Kong Limited
A wholly-owned subsidiary and the first-ever offshore trade finance entity of Polygon Bank, it was started in 2019 with the objective of establishing its footprint in Hong Kong, a vital economic partner of Bangladesh. It plays a key role in facilitating Bangladeshi export into these markets, leveraging Hong Kong’s position as a global financing hub.
#### Polygon Credit Bureau PLC
It is a subsidiary of Polygon Bank, newly established to strengthen the private credit infrastructure in Bangladesh. As one of five firms granted preliminary approval by the central bank, CCB aims to serve as a private credit reporting agency, analyzing borrower data to support more accurate risk assessment for lenders.

#### Eligibility
Offshore Banking (OBU) Account 

Person resident outside Bangladesh including NRBs
Foreign nationals
Persons of Bangladeshi origin (Bangladeshi holding foreign citizenship)
Companies/Firms registered and operating abroad
Foreign institutional investors
Type A units in EPZs (Export Processing Zones, Private EPZs, Economic Zones and Hi-tech parks) in Bangladesh

Note: All individuals will have to be of age 18 & above.

International Banking (IB) Account

The following individuals or entities having bonafide relations with non-residents can open/operate International Banking (IB) Accounts:
 

Any Resident Bangladeshi individual (aged 18 years & above)
Bangladeshi Corporates/Firms
Type A, Type B, Type C industrial enterprises operating in EPZs (Export Processing Zones)/EZs (Economic Zones)/HTPs (High Tech Parks)

#### Required Documents

Individual Account

Copy of valid photo ID of applicant, i.e., passport, respective foreign country’s ID, etc.
If the photo ID is a Bangladeshi passport, additional documents such as resident visa, foreign resident card (i.e., green card), work permit, driving license (issued by foreign authority) or any other document supporting the residency status abroad is required.
Passport sized photograph: applicant – 2 copies, and nominee 1 copy attested by applicant.
Address verification document required only if the declared address is different from the address in the applicant’s Photo ID document. This can be utility bill, driving license (issued by foreign authority) etc.

Non-Individual Account

Extract of Resolution/Board Resolution
Trade License or similar document
Certificate of Incorporation
Memorandum and Articles of Association
Updated Schedule X & Form XII or any recent document that proves shareholding information and names of the Board of Directors with respective designation.
Photo identification of shareholders (Top 5 shareholders’ details required when shareholders are more than 5. If the shareholders are less than 5, then all shareholders’/ all authorized signatories’ for the account/ the beneficial owners’ of the Company)
2 copy passport size photo of each shareholder (Top 5 shareholders’ details required when shareholders are more than 5. If the shareholders are less than 5, then all shareholders’/ all authorized signatories’ for the account/ the beneficial owners’ of the Company)

 

Individual Account

Copy of Valid Photo ID for account holder/ Applicant and Nominee (Such as Passport / National ID/ Birth Certificate etc.)
Passport size Photograph of the Applicant – 2 Copy, Nominee duly attested by the applicant – 1 Copy.
Address Verification Documents if Address not Matched with Applicant Photo ID e.g. Utility Bill etc.
An Undertaking Form duly signed by the Remitter. This will be required before crediting any foreign currency into the account.

Non-Individual Account

Trade License or alternative document
Certificate of Incorporation
Updated Form X & XII
Photo Identification of Shareholder (Top 5 Shareholder's details when Share Holder is more than 5. If the shareholder is less than 5 then all shareholder’s/All authorized Signatories for the account/The beneficial owners of the Company)
2 copy Passport size Photo of Shareholder (Top 5 Shareholder's details when Share Holder is more than 5. If the shareholder is less than 5 then all shareholder’s/All authorized Signatories for the account/The beneficial owners of the Company) 

#### Frequently Asked Questions OBU Account
1. What is Offshore Banking Account?

Account that can be opened by Residents outside Bangladesh in foreign currency. For example: Non-resident Bangladeshi nationals (NRBs), Persons of Bangladeshi origin (Bangladeshi holding foreign citizenship), Foreign nationals, Companies/Firms registered and operating abroad, Foreign institutional investors etc. can open this OBU account.

2. Who can open OBU Account?

Foreign nationals

3. Can customers open a joint OBU account?

Yes, customers can open a joint OBU account. But in such cases both the account holders must individually qualify to open the account as per question 1 or 2 above.

4. What type of account can a customer open?

A customer can open a Fixed Deposit account in foreign currency (USD or EUR) under this program. To credit the funds for the Fixed Deposit account, the customer has to open a current account first. This current account will help to facilitate fund transfers, remittances and other account operations. So, basically the customer will have to have both Current and Fixed deposit account(s) in OBU.

5. Documents required to open OBU account

Document check list for Individuals

Document check list for OBU account for companies/firms registered and operating abroad.

Certificate of Incorporation
Photo identification of shareholders
(Top 5 shareholders’ details required when shareholders are more than 5. If the shareholders are less than 5, then all shareholders’/ all authorized signatories’ for the account/ the beneficial owners’ of the Company)
2 copy passport size photo of each shareholder

6. Is there a physical OBU account opening form?

For individual OBU account opening no physical form will be required. Customer can open account online through the customer service portal in our website.

If the customer is physically present at branch, they can use the regular account opening form. For institutional accounts, the customer can download the form here.

7. Can an existing Polygon Bank customer open OBU account?

Yes, any existing Polygon Bank customer can open OBU account if he or she qualifies for OBU account as per question 1 or 2 above.

8. In which currency can a customer open OBU account?

Currently at Polygon Bank, a customer can open OBU account in USD & EUR currency only.

9. Is there any minimum deposit threshold required to open the account?

For maintaining a current account, no minimum balance is required. For Fixed deposit account the minimum deposit amount is USD/EUR 5,000.

10. What are the tenors available to open Fixed deposit account?

The available tenors are 3 months, 6 months, 1 year, 2 years, 3 years, 4 years and 5 years.

11. How will the customer use the balance of the current account?

The balance of the account can be used for any of the following purposes:

Transferring to on-shore banking BDT accounts to meet necessary payments and investments.
Making outward remittances of the entire balance as and when necessary.

12. Can a customer open a Fixed deposit account without opening a current account?

To open a Fixed deposit account, opening a current account is a mandatory requirement.

13. Is nominee mandatory for OBU account?

Yes, nominee is required for Individual accounts (both current and Fixed deposit) only.

14. Can a customer add multiple nominees to the OBU account?

Yes, the customer can add multiple nominees to the account. For each nominee, we will need photo identification document and one copy passport sized photograph.

15. Can a customer open OBU account in any branch?

Yes, the customer can physically come to open OBU account in any Polygon Bank branch during his/her visit to Bangladesh.

16. Is there any maximum deposit limit for OBU account?

No, there is no maximum limit for OBU account.

17. Can a customer avail Credit card or Debit card against OBU account?

Since the OBU account is opened for the purpose of Fixed Deposit only, the bank will not issue any debit/credit card against this account. 

18. Can a customer open multiple currency OBU account?

Yes, a customer can open OBU accounts in both USD and EUR as per their requirement.

19. Can a customer open OBU account in different banks?

Yes, a customer is free to open multiple OBU accounts in any bank they desire.

20. Can a customer open OBU account in Islamic module?

No, currently this option is not available with Polygon Bank.

#### Frequently Asked Questions IB Account
Account Opening:

2. Who can open OBU Account?

Foreign nationals

Certificate of Incorporation

 

Account Operations:
 

1. How can the account holder credit funds in his account?

Fund can be credited by the account holder through foreign inward remittances received through official banking channels only.

2. Who can send funds (originator) to the OBU account?

Anyone can send funds to the OBU account from any country. There are no restrictions on who can send funds. But the Fixed Deposit account will be opened in the name of the current account holder (Beneficiary).

For example: The current account is opened in the name Mr. Alam.  Mr. Alam can remit funds from abroad to his account. Also anyone else like Mr. Hasan or Mr. Ahmed both can send funds to the account of Mr. Alam. But the Fixed deposit account will be in the name of Mr. Alam only. 

3. Is there any cheque book facility available against OBU account?

No, there is no cheque book facility available. The account can be operated by sending remittance or transfer instructions to the bank.

4. Is it possible to deposit Dollar/EUR in cash form?

Cash deposit is not possible in OBU account. Funds can only be credited by inward remittances received through official channels.

5. Is it possible to withdraw any amount from the account in cash form?

Cash withdrawal is not possible from OBU account.

6. Are OBU customers allowed to avail PolygonTouch, the internet banking app of Polygon Bank?

Yes, PolygonTouch can be used by Individual account holders for viewing purpose only. No transaction can be done through PolygonTouch. They may use it for sending some instructions to the bank.

7. Is there any limit on the number of transactions to a single OBU account?

No there is no limit on the number of transactions in the account.

8. What is the procedure to transfer the balance from foreign currency OBU account to an onshore local currency (BDT) account?

OBU customers can transfer funds from foreign currency OBU account to any local currency account by applying to the bank through the remittance request form .

09. What is the process for Fixed Deposit Encashment?

You can download the Fixed Deposit Encashment form here and fill out and email the signed form to [Polygon Bank email] from your registered email address.
However, if you have already given instruction for encashment at time of opening the Fixed Deposit in the Fixed Deposit opening Form, the deposit will be encashed as per your instructions.

There are no fees and charges for maintaining OBU account and there is no hidden fee also. Details of the fees and charges are given below:

Deposit Rate:
 

 

 

1. Is OBU account an interest bearing account?

Yes, the bank is offering attractive interest rates on Fixed Deposit accounts. However, Current account is a non-interest bearing account. Please click here to know the current offers of Fixed Deposit accounts.

2. Is interest rate fixed in OBU Fixed deposit account?

The interest rate is based on the benchmark rate of the respective currencies which may change on daily basis. Based on the benchmark rate of the respective currencies, the interest rate may vary.

For the Fixed Deposit, the interest rate on the date of booking will be applicable till the maturity of the deposit. Our prevailing deposit interest rates are available in our website.

3. What is a “Benchmark Rate”?

Benchmark or reference rate is a rate that reflects the cost of borrowing in a particular currency based on international demand and supply.

4. What are the benchmark rates of the respective currencies?

Currency Code

	

Benchmark

US Dollar

	

Secured Overnight
Financing Rate (SOFR)

Euro

	

Euro Short-term Rate (ESTR)

 

5. How can I find out the interest rate for Fixed deposits?

To know the interest rates of different tenors that are applicable today, please click here.

6. What interest rate will be applied for premature encashment of OBU Fixed deposit?

If the deposit is kept for less than 3 months, no interest will be paid. For premature encashment after 3 months, applicable rate will the interest rate corresponding to the nearest/immediate lower tenor’s prevailing published interest rate or the FD booking interest rate, whichever is lower.
For example, if you open a Fixed Deposit for 3 years at 8% and want to encash/close after 1 year, 1 year’s prevailing fixed deposit rate will be applicable. But if at that time the 1 year interest rate is higher than 8%, then it will encashed at 8%.

Fund Transfer:

1. What information is required to send remittances to any Polygon Bank account from abroad?

To send remittances, please use below information for beneficiary-

SWIFT Code of Polygon Bank                 : CIBLBDDH

Bank Name                                       : Polygon Bank

Customer’s OBU Account Number    : ……………….

Account Name                                  : …

2. Through which banks can remittances be sent to Polygon Bank?

You can send remittance from any bank in the world to the account in Polygon Bank.
When you are sending the remittance, if your bank from where you are sending the fund requests for bank account information of Polygon Bank i.e., the correspondent foreign banks (intermediary bank) of Polygon Bank, you can share the bank details from this link for convenience: here.

3. What will be the conversion rate if a customer wants to convert the foreign currency balance to BDT to an on-shore bank account?

The bank will use the prevailing TT clean Buying rate of the bank on the date of the conversion. The exchange rates are available here

4. Is the balance in OBU account freely transferable abroad?

Yes, the entire balance of OBU account is freely transferable abroad. Please find remittance request form here

5. If I have OBU account in one currency, can I send outward remittance in other currency?

Yes, you can. In such cases the cross currency conversion will take place.

6. Can customers transfer the balance of OBU account to another of his OBU account?

Yes, the customer can transfer the balance of OBU account to another OBU account.

7. Is remittance incentive paid by the government applicable for OBU deposits?

No, remittance incentive is not applicable to OBU deposits or encashment.

8. What is the SWIFT code of Polygon Bank?

Our SWIFT Code is CIBLBDDH. It is also known as BIC (Bank Identification Code).

Tax & Excise Duty:
 

1. Is there any tax or any duty applicable on the deposit or interest?

As per new Offshore Banking Act passed by National Parliament of Bangladesh on 14 March 2024, depositors are exempted from any tax on the balance of OBU account and on any interest paid. Also no excise duty is applicable on the balance of the account.

General & Others:

 

1. How is Polygon Bank offering such high-interest rates on OBU account deposits?

Offshore banking basically is dependent on foreign borrowings @ 2%-3% margin on SOFR/benchmark rates. Polygon Bank aims to expand its long-term customer deposit base by providing competitive deposit rates aligned with regulatory guidelines. Therefore, Polygon Bank is offering these competitive rates instead of borrowing from abroad and it will save cost to the bank as well as reduce market dependency.

2. Is there any possibility to obtain Commercially Important Person (CIP) status from government of Bangladesh by maintaining funds in an OBU account?

Since this is an investment in an OBU account, this is out of the scope of non-resident remittance category which is considered for CIP status by the government.

#### Insurance Benefit
Double Benefit Insurance (DBI): Double Benefit Insurance (DBI) insures your outstanding balance during unfortunate events of death or permanent total disability (PTD) by paying off your full outstanding balance and an equal amount to your nominee. The maximum payout amount will not exceed BDT 2,000,000 or twice the outstanding amount, whichever is lower. Once you get enrolled in the program, you will be charged a premium of 0.40% on your monthly card outstanding balance.

 Accidental Death Insurance (AADI): All Accidental Death Insurance (AADI): A Cardmember automatically receives All Accidental Death Insurance free of cost. This covers death due to any accident. The coverage is BDT 500,000 for each Cardmember, which will be paid to the Cardmember’s nominee in case of an accidental death.

 Critical Care Insurance (CCI): The Critical Care Insurance program allows Cardmembers to receive benefit of treatment of 18 different critical diseases. #### FAQ
1. What is the Freelancer Deposit Product of Polygon Bank?

The Freelancer Deposit Product is a bundled account solution designed for Bangladeshi freelancers to receive, retain, and utilize foreign inward remittances in compliance with the prevailing regulations and guidelines of Bangladesh Bank. The product facilitates the formal banking of freelancing income, including incentive-eligible foreign remittances, through a structured combination of local and foreign currency accounts.

2. Where can customers open the Freelancer Deposit Product accounts?

Customers may open the Freelancer Deposit Product at all branches and sub-branches of Polygon Bank across Bangladesh, subject to fulfillment of applicable documentation, verification, and regulatory requirements.

3. What accounts are included in the Freelancer Deposit Product?

The Freelancer Deposit Product consists of the following two accounts:
a) Freelancer Savings Account (BDT), which is an interest-bearing savings account maintained in local currency.
b) Freelancer ERQ Account (USD), which is a non-interest-bearing foreign currency current account.

4.Who is eligible to avail the Freelancer Deposit Product?

Any Bangladeshi individual who is at least 18 years of age, resides in Bangladesh, and holds a valid Freelancer ID is eligible to avail the Freelancer Deposit Product, subject to compliance with Bangladesh Bank regulations and Polygon Bank’s internal policies.

5. What documents are required to open the Freelancer Deposit Product?

To open the Freelancer Deposit Product with Polygon Bank, the following documents are required, subject to regulatory verification and the Bank’s internal compliance requirements:
For the account holder:
•    Valid photo identification document (NID/Birth certificate/Passport)
•    Two recent passport-size photographs
•    Valid Freelancer ID
For the nominee:
•    Valid photo identification document
•    One recent passport-size photograph
All documents must be valid, complete, and acceptable to the Bank as per prevailing regulations.

6. What is a Freelancer ID card and where can a customer obtain it?

A Freelancer ID is an official identification that certifies an individual’s status as a freelancer. Freelancer IDs issued by the Government of Bangladesh through the ICT Division are accepted for availing the Freelancer Deposit Product. In addition, Freelancer ID cards issued by the Bangladesh Freelancer Development Society (BFDS) are also accepted, subject to verification and regulatory compliance.

7. Is joint operation allowed for Freelancer accounts?

No. Both the Freelancer Savings Account and the Freelancer ERQ Account are strictly maintained as single-operation individual accounts. Joint operation is not permitted under this product.

8. Is there any minimum balance requirement to open the accounts?

No initial deposit or minimum opening balance is required to open either the Freelancer Savings Account or the Freelancer ERQ Account.

9. How is interest calculated on the Freelancer Savings Account?

Interest on the Freelancer Savings Account is calculated on a EOD balance and credited monthly, in accordance with the prevailing interest rate approved by ALCO. Interest is payable only if the end-of-day balance is at least BDT 10,000, subject to applicable regulations.

10. What is the interest rate of the Freelancer Savings Account?

The interest rate applicable to the Freelancer Savings Account is determined by Polygon Bank in line with its prevailing deposit rate structure and may be revised from time to time. The latest and applicable interest rates for the Freelancer Savings Account are published on Polygon Bank’s official website and can be accessed through the following link:
[Polygon Bank website]

11. Does Freelancer ERQ Account earn any interest?

No. The Freelancer ERQ Account is a non-interest-bearing foreign currency current account and does not earn any interest.

12. What is an ERQ account?

ERQ stands for Exporters’ Retention Quota. An ERQ account is a foreign currency account permitted under Bangladesh Bank regulations that allows eligible exporters, including freelancers, to retain a prescribed portion of their inward foreign remittances in foreign currency for approved purposes.

13. What are the benefits of the Freelancer ERQ Account?

The Freelancer ERQ Account allows freelancers to retain a portion of eligible inward foreign remittances in foreign currency in accordance with Bangladesh Bank regulations. Funds held in the ERQ account may be used for bona fide business expenses and permissible personal expenses through approved banking channels. The balance may also be used through an international debit card for eligible foreign currency transactions or converted into local currency at an Authorized Dealer branch at the prevailing exchange rate, subject to regulatory requirements.

14. Are all freelancing income funds retained in the Freelancer ERQ Account?

No. For inward remittances received from freelancing platforms approved by Bangladesh Bank under FE Circular No. 07 dated 30 January 2022, a prescribed portion may be retained in the ERQ Account, while the remaining portion is encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account. Freelancing income is not retained in full in the Freelancer ERQ Account. Retention is permitted only up to the portion prescribed by Bangladesh Bank, while the remaining amount is mandatorily encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account in local currency.

15. What happens to freelancing income received from different websites or platforms?

All freelancing income is initially credited to the Freelancer ERQ Account. For inward remittances received from freelancing platforms approved by Bangladesh Bank under FE Circular No. 07 dated 30 January 2022, a prescribed portion may be retained in the ERQ Account, while the remaining portion is encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account. Freelancing income received from other websites or platforms that are not listed as Bangladesh Bank-approved platforms is fully encashed and transferred to the linked Freelancer Savings Account, with no retention permitted in the ERQ Account.

16. What conversion rate is applied when funds are transferred to the Freelancer Savings Account?

The prevailing exchange rate of the day is applied when foreign currency funds are transferred from the Freelancer ERQ Account to the linked Freelancer Savings Account. The daily exchange rate is published on the official website of Polygon Bank.

17. What portion of freelancing income can be retained in the Freelancer ERQ Account?

As per prevailing Bangladesh Bank regulations, currently 35 percent of eligible freelancing income may be retained in the Freelancer ERQ Account. The remaining 65 percent is encashed and credited to the linked Freelancer Savings Account in local currency. This ratio is subject to change based on future regulatory directives.

18. How can funds in the Freelancer Savings Account be used?

Funds in the Freelancer Savings Account may be used in the same manner as a regular savings account, including deposits, withdrawals, and fund transfers, subject to the laws of Bangladesh and applicable regulations. The associated debit card may be endorsed for foreign currency usage and used for permissible international transactions.

19. Can a customer open Fixed Deposit or DPS from the Freelancer Savings Account?

Yes. A customer may open term deposit products such as Fixed Deposit or DPS using funds from the Freelancer Savings Account, in the same manner as applicable for regular Savings or Current Accounts. Such term deposits will be governed by Polygon Bank’s standard product terms, conditions, and prevailing policies.

20. Are debit cards available with the Freelancer accounts?

Yes. A dual-currency debit card is available with the Freelancer Savings Account, and the first debit card issued under the product is provided free of charge for lifetime. An international debit card is available with the Freelancer ERQ Account for permissible foreign currency transactions. A valid passport is required to avail this card, and the card is also issued free of charge for lifetime.

21. Are cheque books available for Freelancer accounts?

Cheque book facilities are available for the Freelancer Savings Account only. Cheque books are not available for the Freelancer ERQ Account.

22. Are PolygonTouch and SMS services available for Freelancer accounts?

Yes. PolygonTouch internet banking and SMS alert services are available for both Freelancer accounts. PolygonTouch functionality for the Freelancer ERQ Account is limited. SMS charges for the Freelancer Savings Account apply as per the prevailing Schedule of Charges, while SMS service for the Freelancer ERQ Account is provided free of charge.

23. Will tax on interest and excise duties be applied to the Freelancer accounts?

Yes. Applicable taxes, excise duties, VAT, and other statutory charges are applied in accordance with the prevailing laws and regulations of the Government of Bangladesh and directives issued by Bangladesh Bank. Any tax on interest earned is deducted at source as per applicable regulations.

24. Are remittance certificates provided to Freelancer customers?

Yes. Four remittance certificates are issued free of charge each year on a quarterly basis and are sent to the customer’s registered mailing address.

#### Frequently Asked Question
What is the Student Savings Account – School Plan?
It is a savings account designed for Bangladeshi students below 18 years of age. The account helps students develop savings habits and access basic banking services from an early age.

Who is eligible to open this account?
Any Bangladeshi student below 18 years of age is eligible to open a Student Savings Account – School Plan.

Who will operate the account?
As the student is below 18 years of age, the account will be operated by the student’s parent or legal guardian in accordance with the Bank’s applicable procedures.

Is any minimum opening balance required?
No. There is no minimum balance requirement for opening the account.

What documents are required from the student?
The following documents are generally required:

Student’s NID, birth certificate or valid passport
Recent photograph
Student ID card or tuition fee slip issued within the last six months

Where the student has only a birth certificate without a photograph and does not have any valid photo ID, a certification letter from the relevant educational institution must be submitted. The student’s photograph must be attested by the principal, head teacher or an authorized official of the institution.

What documents are required from the parent or legal guardian?
The parent or legal guardian must generally provide:

Valid NID, passport or driving license
Recent photograph
Applicable source-of-fund documents

What is the monthly deposit limit?
According to Student Banking Guidelines of Bangladesh Bank, maximum of BDT 25,000 may be deposited into the account in a month.

What is the maximum account balance?
According to Student Banking Guidelines of Bangladesh Bank, the account balance must not exceed BDT 300,000 at any time.

Is a debit card available with the account?
Yes. A debit card may be issued in the student’s name.

What is the debit card transaction limit?
According to Student Banking Guidelines of Bangladesh Bank, the standard monthly transaction limit is BDT 15,000 for withdrawals through ATM, POS and permitted online channels.

The limit may be increased to a maximum of BDT 25,000 upon the request of the parent or legal guardian, subject to the Bank’s approval and applicable procedures.

Can debit card be issued in the parent’s or guardian’s name?
Debit card will be issued in the name of the parent or legal guardian. The debit card facility will be provided to the eligible student account holder in accordance with the Bank’s policy.

Is a cheque book available with the account?
No. Cheque book facility is not available with the Student Savings Account – School Plan.

Is PolygonTouch available with the account?
No. PolygonTouch facility is not available with this account.

Yes. SMS alerts will be sent to the registered mobile number of the student’s parent or legal guardian.

Can a DPS be opened from this account?
Yes. A Deposit Pension Scheme, or DPS, may be opened from the Student Savings Account – School Plan, subject to the Bank’s applicable product terms.

What is the maximum monthly DPS instalment amount?
The monthly DPS instalment may be up to BDT 20,000, subject to the Bank’s applicable product rules.

Can an FDR or Monthly Interest Paying FD be opened from this account?
No. Only DPS may be opened from the Student Savings Account – School Plan.

Who can be nominated for the account?
Only the student’s parent or legal guardian may be nominated for the account.

Can a student maintain more than one School Plan account?
No. A student may maintain only one Student Savings Account – School Plan with Polygon Bank.

What happens when the student reaches 18 years of age?
The account will be placed under debit freeze when the student reaches 18 years of age.

The account holder must visit Polygon Bank Branch or Sub Branch to regularize or close the account. To regularize the account, the account holder must provide an updated identification document and recent photograph and complete the required full KYC process.

After regularization, the account may be converted into a Student Savings Account – College Plan or another eligible savings account.

#### Frequently Asked Question
What is the Student Savings Account – College Plan?
It is a savings account designed for Bangladeshi students aged between 18 and 25 years. The account provides regular banking, transaction and savings facilities suitable for college and university students.

Bangladeshi students aged between 18 and 25 years are eligible to open a Student Savings Account – College Plan, subject to submission of the required documents.

Can the account be opened as a single account?
Yes. The account may be opened as a single account in the student’s name.

Can the account be opened jointly?
Yes. The account may be opened jointly by the student with a parent or another family member.

Student’s NID, birth certificate, valid passport or driving licence
Recent photograph

Are documents required from a joint account holder or beneficial owner?
Yes. Information and identification documents of the joint account holder or beneficial owner must be provided where applicable.

Additional documents, including source-of-fund documents, may also be required in accordance with the Bank’s KYC requirements.

Yes. Debit card facility is available with the Student Savings Account – College Plan.

What is the debit card charge?
The debit card is free for the first year. From the second year onward, a 50% waiver will apply to the regular debit card charge.

Yes. Cheque book facility is available with the account.

What is the cheque book charge?
A 50% waiver will apply to the regular cheque book charge.

Yes. PolygonTouch facility is available in accordance with the Bank’s applicable digital banking policy.

Yes. SMS Alert facility is available and is free of charge.

What are the deposit and withdrawal limits?
Deposit and withdrawal limits will be determined based on the student’s or beneficial owner’s KYC profile and Transaction Profile, or TP, in accordance with the Bank’s applicable policy.

Can Term Deposit be opened from this account?
Yes. DPS, FDR and Monthly Interest Paying FD may be opened from the Student Savings Account – College Plan, subject to the Bank’s applicable product terms.

Is nomination facility available?
Yes. Nomination facility is available in accordance with the Bank’s prevailing nomination policy.

Can a student maintain more than one College Plan account?
No. A student may maintain only one Student Savings Account – College Plan with Polygon Bank.

Can a School Plan account be converted into a College Plan account?
Yes. Once the student becomes eligible, an existing Student Savings Account – School Plan may be converted into a Student Savings Account – College Plan after completion of the required KYC and account regularization process.

Can a student open a new College Plan account while maintaining a School Plan account?
No. The existing School Plan account must first be converted or closed before a new Student Savings Account – College Plan can be opened.

What happens when the account holder reaches 25 years of age?
The account will be placed under debit freeze when the account holder reaches 25 years of age.

The account holder must visit Polygon Bank Branch or Sub Branch to regularize or close the account. To regularize the account, the account holder must complete the required full KYC process.

After regularization, the account may be converted into another eligible savings account.

#### FAQ
1. What is the Insurance-Backed DPS?

The Insurance-Backed DPS is a monthly deposit scheme where a customer deposits a fixed amount for a selected tenure, and in the event of the depositor’s death during the DPS period, the nominee receives the full insured amount as per the policy.

2. Who can open an Insurance-Backed DPS?

Any Bangladeshi citizen aged 18 to 55 years who maintains a Savings or Current Deposit account with Polygon Bank is eligible to open this DPS.

3. Where can the account be opened?

The account can be opened at any branch or sub-branch of Polygon Bank.

4. What tenures are available?

Tenures of 5, 10, 15, and 20 years are available. The maximum tenor depends on the age of the applicant as well.

Tenor

	

Maximum Age of the Customer

5 Years

	

Below 55 Years

10 Years

	

Below 50 Years

15 Years

	

Below 45 Years

20 Years

	

Below 40 Years

 

5. What is the monthly installment amount?

The monthly installment amount can range from BDT 500 to BDT 10,000 and must be in multiples of BDT 500.

6. Is insurance coverage included?

Yes. The DPS includes insurance coverage from the date of opening, and the bank pays the insurance premium on behalf of the customer.

7. What happens if the account holder dies during the DPS period?

If the account holder dies during the DPS period, the nominee receives the full matured amount, and the DPS cannot be continued by the nominee.

8. Who can be assigned as the nominee?

The nominee must be the account holder’s parent, spouse, or child. If the nominee is a minor, a legal guardian must also be assigned.

9. Can multiple nominees be added?

Yes.

10. Can the nominee be changed later?

Yes. The nominee may be changed at any time before maturity by submitting a written instruction to the bank.

 

 

11. How are monthly installments collected?

Installments are collected through auto-debit from the customer’s linked Savings or Current Deposit account. Cash cannot be deposited directly into the DPS account.

12. What happens if a monthly installment is missed?

If an installment is missed, charges will be applied according to the Schedule of Charges. If three consecutive installments remain unpaid, the DPS will be closed automatically after notifying the customer.

13. Can a customer open more than one Insurance-Backed DPS?

Yes. Customers may open multiple DPS accounts under this scheme.

14. Is a loan facility available against this DPS?

Yes. After completing one year of the DPS, a customer may obtain a loan up to 90% of the deposited amount.

15. What happens if the account is closed before maturity?

If the account is closed within the first year, the customer will receive only the deposited amount; no interest will be paid, and the insurance premium paid by the bank will be deducted. If the account is closed after one year but before maturity, the customer will receive the deposited amount with savings interest, and the insurance premium paid by the bank will be deducted.

16. Under what circumstances is a medical examination mandatory?

A medical examination is required when the maturity value exceeds BDT 7,50,000, or when the customer’s age exceeds 50 years.

17. What types of medical examinations are required?

18. Is there a designated list of hospitals?

No. Customers may complete medical tests at any reputed hospital or any renowned diagnostic center in Bangladesh.

19. When should customers submit their medical reports?

Medical reports must be submitted at the time of opening the DPS.

20. Who will pay for the required medical tests?

The customer will initially pay for the medical tests. Once the DPS is opened and reports are submitted, the insurance company will reimburse the expenses as per the approved reimbursement rates.

21. Is there a reimbursement limit for medical examination expenses?

Yes. The reimbursement limits are as follows:

22. What fees or duties apply?

Applicable taxes, excise duty, and regulatory charges will be deducted as per rules.

23. Are cheque books or passbooks issued?

No. Cheque books and passbooks are not issued for this DPS.

24. Can cash be deposited directly into the DPS account?

No. All installments must be collected through auto-debit from the linked account.

25. Within what timeframe must the nominee notify the bank in the event of the account holder’s death?

In the event of the account holder’s death, the nominee must inform the bank within 90 days from the date of death. If the bank is not notified within this stipulated period, the insurance benefit under the scheme will not be applicable.
