#### Frequently Asked Questions
Account Opening

1. What is Resident Foreign Currency Deposit (RFCD) account?

Ans: RFCD is a Deposit account in foreign currency opened and maintained by resident Bangladeshis with the foreign exchange brought in at the time of their return from travel abroad. Only Resident Bangladeshis can open this account.

2. Who can open RFCD account?

Ans: Any Bangladeshi resident aged over 18 years can open Resident Foreign Currency Deposit (RFCD) account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 21)

3. Can a foreign national open an RFCD account?

Ans: No. RFCD account is for only resident Bangladeshis. However, foreign nationals can open other foreign currency accounts. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 21)

4. What are the required documents to open an RFCD account?

Ans: The required documents are:

Passport size photograph -2 copies of account holder, 1 copy of nominee
Valid Bangladeshi Passport (information page)
PSR or Proof of Submission of Return – tax return of previous financial year mandatory for balance equivalent to BDT 10 lacs or more
Nominee photo ID – NID /Birth certificate/Passport

Note: At the time of depositing any cash foreign currency into the account, we will need the original valid Bangladeshi Passport to check the travel dates i.e., Arrival seal pages of the journey against which deposit is being made. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

5. What is the account type of RFCD account?

Ans: It is an interest-bearing deposit account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 25)

6. Can a customer open a joint RFCD account?

Ans: No, a customer cannot open a joint RFCD account, but the customer can provide two supplementary debit or credit card to his dependents against the account. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

7. Can customer add nominee to the RFCD account?

Ans: Yes, nominee(s) can be added to the RFCD account.

8. Is it possible to open/deposit RFCD account if the passport is expired?

Ans: No. Current valid passport is required to open or make any deposit to RFCD account. Additionally, proof of foreign travel i.e., the visa and arrival seal on the valid passport is required to be submitted for any credit.

9. In which currencies can an individual open an RFCD account in Polygon Bank?

Ans: Currently, we can open RFCD account in USD, GBP & EURO. i.e. U.S Dollar, Pound & Euro (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December, 03, 2023)

10. Can customer open RFCD account in any branch?

Ans: Customers can place account opening request at any of our branches.  However, the account will be opened at an AD branch of the bank.  (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

11. What is the minimum account opening balance of RFCD account?

Ans: USD 500 or equivalent. This, however, can be waived with proper approvals at branch level. 

12. Can a customer open multiple RFCD accounts in a bank?

Ans: Yes, a customer can open multiple RFCD accounts but in different currencies in a bank.

13. Can a Non-resident Bangladeshi open RFCD account?

Ans: No. Only resident Bangladeshi individual is allowed to open an RFCD account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 21)

14. Is it possible to open RFCD account without foreign travel?

Ans: Yes. But in this case, no deposits will be allowed at the time of account opening. Any deposit requires proof of foreign travel. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

Account Operations

1. What are the options of deposit in the RFCD account?

Ans: A customer can deposit Cash foreign currency in the RFCD account.

Cash foreign currency Deposit 

Any amount up to USD 10,000 or equivalent per trip can be deposited without any declaration. However, if the travel took place before 03-02-2020, the amount is USD 5,000 per trip. 

If the amount is above USD 10,000 per trip, then a declaration to Customs Authorities in Form FMJ while entering the country, will be required to be submitted and the deposit must be made within 30 days of arrival. (Bangladesh Bank Press release DCP(PR)05/2022-301)

In all cases of deposit, customers must fill out Polygon Bank’s RFCD declaration form mentioning the travel details.

2. Is there any time limit for depositing cash foreign currency in RFCD Account?

Ans: No, opening an RFCD account does not have to be related to any travel.  A customer can open RFCD account any time with a current valid passport. But to credit any fund against any travel, the travel must be undertaken with the current valid passport.  The bank will not credit any funds against a travel which was undertaken by the customer previously and that particular passport has expired. 

However, foreign currency has to be deposited within 30 days of arrival for deposits which require declaration on Form FMJ as stated in question no.1 above. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

3. What is an FMJ form?

Ans: FMJ form is a 3-page Customs Declaration Form for bringing cash foreign currency above USD 10,000 into the country. It must be availed and declared at the airport while crossing Customs check.

4. Is cash foreign currency endorsement from RFCD Account a part of Yearly Travel Quota?

Ans: No. Cash currency endorsement limit from RFCD Account and Yearly Travel Quota are totally separate. 

5. How much can the foreign cash currency be endorsed from RFCD account?

Ans: Maximum cash of USD 5,000 can be endorsed from an RFCD Account per person per trip. If customer wishes, they can also cash endorse the remaining RFCD Account balance in other currency (subject to availability). (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 22)

6. What is the limit of endorsement of other foreign cash currency (not USD) from RFCD account?

Ans: There is no limit. The total available balance in RFCD Account can be taken in cash in foreign currencies other than USD (subject to availability) against endorsement while traveling. 

7. Can a customer endorse cash USD 5,000 from their RFCD Account and in addition endorse USD 5,000 from their yearly Travel Quota (a total of cash USD 10,000) for the same trip?

Ans: No, in that case the customer can avail either one for each trip. Meaning, the maximum limit is USD 5,000 cash currency endorsement per trip. He can avail from both the entitlements but not in the same trip. 

8. Can a customer endorse their passport in a different currency other than the currency of their RFCD account?

Ans: Yes, a customer can do that. Meaning, if the RFCD account is in USD, he can still endorse in equivalent EUR or any other currency he prefers (subject of availability). 

9. Is it possible to endorse cash foreign currency to the account holder’s dependent’s passport prior to foreign travel?

No. Cash endorsement can be used for account holder’s own travel purposes only.

10. Can a customer withdraw cash from an ATM abroad during a foreign travel from RFCD balance?

Ans: Yes. Customers can withdraw balance from an ATM abroad during a foreign travel.

11. Is there any withdrawal limit for ATMs during foreign travel?

Ans: No, there is no limit from Polygon Bank’s side.  There may be amount restrictions of the foreign bank whose ATM the customer is using. 

12. Is the balance in these accounts freely transferrable abroad?

Ans: Yes. Balances in the RFCD account is freely transferrable abroad. Account holders and their dependents can use it for the purpose of foreign travel, paying tuition fees, medical expenses etc.  (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 22)

13. If the customer has an RFCD account in one currency, can he/she send outward remittance in other currency?

Ans: Yes. In this case, currency conversion will take place. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 22)

14. Is it possible to withdraw BDT from ATM machine using RFCD debit/credit cards?

Ans: Yes. Currently it is possible to withdraw BDT from ATM machines using RFCD debit/credit cards.

15. Is it possible to withdraw and endorse cash foreign currency without upcoming foreign travel?

Ans: No that is not possible. Valid Visa along with travel ticket (travel date within the next 14 days) is mandatory for endorsement. 

16. Can customers transfer the balance of one of their RFCD Account to another of their RFCD Account?

Ans: Balance of RFCD account cannot be transferred to another RFCD account of the same account holder within Polygon Bank or within any other bank.  

17. Can customers transfer the balance of any other FCY Account to their RFCD Account?

Ans: Balance of Foreign Currency account (FCY) cannot be transferred to RFCD account. 

18. What are eligible deposit/credit/inward transactions for RFCD Account?

Ans: RFCD account can only be credited with cash after returning from foreign travel or transfer from other RFCD account of the same customer. However, proceeds of export of goods or services from Bangladesh or commission arising from business deals in Bangladesh cannot be credited to such accounts.  (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

19. Can customers avail any credit card or debit card against RFCD account?

Ans: Customers can avail credit card and debit card against the account. Moreover, dependents of the customer can avail maximum of two supplementary credit card and debit card in their names. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

20. Is cheque book facility available in RFCD account?

Ans: Cheque book is not available in RFCD account.

21. Is it possible to receive remittance in RFCD account?

Remittance is not allowed in RFCD account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 23)

22. In which purpose the RFCD account balance can be used?

Ans: RFCD account balance can be used to remit outside for the purpose of travelling abroad, education expenditure, medical expenditure and any other purpose.  

The entire balance held in RFCD Account can be easily converted to BDT. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

23. Can the balance be used for e-commerce transactions and is there any limit on transactions?

Ans: Yes. Balance of RFCD accounts can be used for e-commerce transactions. There is no cap or maximum celling for e-commerce transactions. (FE Circular Letter No. 05, May, 09, 2024)

24. Can a customer deposit multiple times against the same travel?

Ans: Yes, a customer can deposit multiple times against the same travel but within the permissible limit per trip. 

25. Can a customer open a Fixed Deposit with the balance of RFCD account?

Ans: No, a customer cannot open a Fixed Deposit with the balance of RFCD account.

26. Are there any supplementary cards available?

Ans: Yes, a maximum total of two supplementary cards (Debit or Credit) can be availed by dependents of the account holder. (FE Circular Letter No. 19, December 03, 2023)

27. What Credit Cards can customers avail against RFCD account?

Ans: Customers can open RFCD Backed Credit Card and Zero Limit Credit Card (where balance can be transferred from account)

28. What is an RFCD Backed Credit Card?

Ans: Customer’s preferred RFCD account balance will be placed under “Lien”, and that equivalent balance will be available as limit for the RFCD Backed Credit Card. Essentially, works like a secured credit card.

29. What is a Zero Limit Credit Card?

Ans: Customer’s preferred RFCD account balance will be transferred to the Zero Limit Credit Card. Balance will be available as the limit for the Card. Essentially, works like a prepaid credit card.

Interest Rate

1. Is the RFCD account an interest-bearing account?

Ans: Yes, RFCD is an Interest-bearing account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 25) 

2. What is the interest rate and how will the customer know the prevailing interest rate?

Ans: The interest rate is decided by Polygon Bank and found in bank’s website at the following link: [Polygon Bank website] \

(FE Circular No 28, Dated October 31, 2024)

3. Is interest rate fixed in RFCD account?

Ans: Ans: No. The interest rate is not fixed. Polygon Bank may update the rate from time to time.

4. How frequently is the interest calculated?

Ans: Interest is calculated daily on daily end of day account balance.

5. How frequent is the interest paid?

Ans: Interest is credited quarterly in customer’s account.

6. Are there any interest eligibility criteria for RFCD account?

Ans: Yes. If the account balance of a customer goes below GPB 500, USD 1,000 or equivalent currency for another currency account in any single day, customer will not be eligible to earn interest for that month.

Equivalent currency of USD 1,000 for EURO will be determined through mid-rate conversion.

 

 

Tax & Excise Duty

1. Is tax applicable on the interest of RFCD account?

Ans: Yes. Tax is applicable and is deducted at Source (TDS) at the time of credit to the account. 

2. How much is the TDS deducted?

Ans: At the rate of 10% if the customer has submitted PSR (Proof of Submission of Return) for the previous financial year to the bank, otherwise, at the rate of 15%. 

3. Is Excise Duty applicable?

Ans: Yes, Excise Duty is applicable on RFCD account balance.

#### What is Insurance benefit ?

 

Accidental Death Insurance (AADI): All Accidental Death Insurance (AADI): A cardmember automatically receives All Accidental Death Insurance free of cost. This covers death due to any accident. The coverage is BDT 500,000 for each cardmember, which will be paid to the cardmember’s nominee in case of accidental death.

 

Critical Care Insurance (CCI): The Critical Care Insurance program allows cardmembers to receive benefit of treatment of 18 critical diseases. For the AMEX Platinum Card, the maximum coverage cardmembers can avail per year under the Critical Care program is BDT 300,000.
The Critical Care Insurance program allows Cardmembers to receive benefit of treatment of 18 different critical diseases:

#### How can the Wristband and Strap loop be used?
The Wristband can be worn on the wrist and the Strap loop can be attached to your watch strap. With the PolygonGo chip inserted into either of these, you can make seamless NFC payments on the go with a tap.

#### Who are eligible for a PolygonGo?
Customers with a Polygon Bank consumer VISA Credit Card or Current/Savings account are eligible to apply for a PolygonGo.
Note: Foreign Currency cards or Foreign Currency accounts are not eligible.

#### What is PolygonGo?
PolygonGo is the latest payment technology chip from Polygon Bank. The PolygonGo chip can be inserted into the Wristband or Strap loop to easily make NFC payments with a tap.

#### How can someone apply for a PolygonGo?
Customers can apply for a PolygonGo through our branches, card service centers or sales channel.

#### What are the color choices for the Wristbands?
The Wristband can be chosen from three colors; Black, Green and Orange.
Note: Color choices are subject to availability.

#### What are the color choices for the Strap loop?
The Strap loop is available in black color only.

#### Can the Wristband or Strap loop be purchased separately?
No. The Wristband and Strap loop has to be purchased together. Customer can use either according to his/her convenience

#### How to use a PolygonGo?
Customers can start using a PolygonGo by following these simple steps:

Step 1: Remove PolygonGo chip from the chip holder.
Step 2: Activate the PolygonGo chip and generate PIN through Smart IVR/IVR/PolygonTouch Digital Banking.
Step 3: Insert the PolygonGo chip into your Wristband or Strap loop.
Step 4: The PolygonGo device is now ready to make NFC payments.

#### What is included in the PolygonGo box?
The box includes:

NFC Wearable Chip
Chip Holder
Wristband
Strap loop
Mailer

#### Why use a PolygonGo?
With PolygonGo you can instantly make NFC payments with one tap. Morover, you can wear it on your wrist or pair it with your watch strap. PolygonGo is a blend of convenience and style making your purchases easier with each transaction.

#### How to do online transactions with your PolygonGo?
Information provided on the chip holder can be used to make online transactions with the PolygonGo.

#### Is there any transaction limit for the PolygonGo?
Maximum 20 NFC transactions can be made per day using your PolygonGo chip.

#### Is there any cashback offer on transactions with the PolygonGo?
Yes, 2% cashback upto 1000 per quarter on every PolygonGo POS transaction. Except Fuel station transactions.
Note: Terms & Conditions Applied

#### Can a PolygonGo be taken without a card?
PolygonGo (Credit) can only be taken by a VISA Credit Card holder. PolygonGo(Debit) can be taken by a Current/Savings account holder, even without a Debit Card.

#### Can new customers apply for a PolygonGo?
Yes. New customers can apply for a PolygonGo along with their application for a new VISA Credit Card or Current/Savings account

#### Which Current accounts can be used to take a PolygonGo?
Individual or Proprietorship current accounts can be used to take a PolygonGo.

#### Can foreign currency transactions be made with the PolygonGo?
Yes. The PolygonGo chip needs to be endorsed before making transactions in USD or USD equivalent foreign currency.
Note: International POS transactions above BDT 5,000 or USD equivalent cannot be made with the PolygonGo chip, without PIN confirmation.

#### What are the allowable modes of usage?
POS, Ecom and Cash By Code.

#### What is the validity of the PolygonGo chip?
The PolygonGo chip will have a validity of 5 years. Upon expiry of the chip after 5 years, it will be auto renewed with a re-issue fee. A new set of the product (including Wristband and Strap loop) will be sent to the customer.

#### Is there any annual fee for the PolygonGo?
There is no annual fee. Customers will be charged with a one-time issuance fee.

#### What to do in case the PolygonGo chip is stolen/lost?
In case of stolen/lost chip, please immediately deactivate the chip through PolygonTouch or by calling [Polygon Bank hotline]. After deactivating the chip, you can request for a replacement through our call center ([Polygon Bank hotline]), terms and conditions applied.

#### What is the replacement process?
If the chip is stolen/lost/damaged/nonfunctional, it can be replaced by a new set of the product (including Wristband and Strap loop) with a replacement fee. Please call [Polygon Bank hotline] for replacement request.

#### What are the fees and charges for the PolygonGo chip?
The following fees and charges shall be applicable:

Fee/Charge Type	PolygonGo (Credit)	PolygonGo (Debit)
Issuance/Re-Issue/Replacement Fee	BDT 3,000	BDT 3,000
Monthly Interest For Purchase1	1.67%	N/A
EMI Interest Rate Monthly	0.92%	N/A
Currency Conversion Markup Fee (Other than USD)2	3%	3%
Monthly Balance transfer interest rate	0.92%	N/A
Minimum due for monthly payment	BDT 500 or 3% whichever is higher	N/A
EMI Early Settlement Fee	0.5 % of the remaining balance	N/A
PIN Regeneration Fee (Call Center Only)	BDT 500	N/A
Fee for Credit Card Payment throughMFS Wallets & Internet Banking Applications3	BDT 35	N/A
Incoming Fund Transfer3	N/A	BDT 15
Processing fee for fund transfer from PolygonTouch to CASA and MFS Wallet	BDT 100 or 2% whichever is higher	N/A
Fee for Fund Loading to MFS Wallet (Add Money)	1%	Free
ATM cash withdrawal Fee (Cash By Code, for Debit Card Only)	N/A	Free
ATM Cash Withdrawal Limit	N/A	As per existing Cash By Code Limit via PolygonTouch

 

Note

15% VAT shall be charged as applicable.
Compound interest shall be charged on all applicable interest.
Flat interest rate shall be used to calculate the interest payable for EMIs.
Maximum interest free period shall be 45 days and minimum shall be 15 days. Interest shall not be charged if you have paid your previous month’s outstanding amount in due time. If you pay partial amount or minimum amount, the interest on your retail transactions and fees shall be calculated from the following day of your last payment due date.
Interest charges are imposed both on the OUTSTANDING RETAIL TRANSACTION BALANCE & FEES that is not paid within the PAYMENT DUE DATE. Interest shall be calculated from the following day of your last payment due date until full payment.

3% additional markup shall be added at the time of settlement of any international transaction except in USD and shall be reflected on the monthly billing statement.
Note: For PolygonGo (Credit) 3% markup fee is inclusive of VAT.

For credit card bill payment using VISA Direct payment gateway or for incoming fund transfer from all MFS wallets/Internet Banking Applications using VISA Direct payment gateway. Charges will be applicable as per existing process.

#### How will the PolygonGo be delivered?
Debit Card PolygonGo taken for a Current/Savings account will be delivered to customer's preferred branch. Customer can then collect it from the branch. Credit Card PolygonGo taken for a VISA Credit Card will be delivered to customer's communication address.

#### How to permanently close a PolygonGo?
A PolygonGo can be permanently closed by visiting your nearest Polygon Bank Plc branch.

#### Can a PolygonGo be used if the VISA card is closed?
PolygonGo taken for your Current/Savings account can be used even if the VISA Debit Card is closed, provided the account is operational. However, PolygonGo taken for your VISA Credit Card cannot be used if the Credit Card is closed.

Insurance benefits:

•   Double Benefit Insurance (DBI): Double Benefit Insurance (DBI) insures your outstanding balance during unfortunate events of death or permanent total disability (PTD) by paying off your full outstanding balance and an equal amount to your nominee. The maximum payout amount will not exceed BDT 2,000,000 or twice the outstanding amount, whichever is lower. Once you get enrolled in the program, you will be charged a premium of 0.40% on your monthly card outstanding balance.

•   Critical Care Insurance (CCI): The Critical Care Insurance program allows cardmembers to receive the benefit of treatment of 18 different critical diseases. For the AMEX Gold Card, the maximum coverage cardmembers would receive per year under the Critical Care program is BDT 200,000.

#### Insurance Benefits
Double Benefit Insurance (DBI)

Provides coverage against the outstanding card balance in the event of death or permanent total disability (PTD). The benefit settles the outstanding balance and pays an equal amount to the nominee.

Maximum payout: BDT 2,000,000 or twice the outstanding balance, whichever is lower
Premium: 0.40% of the monthly outstanding balance

Critical Care Insurance (CCI)

Coverage for 18 critical illnesses, including:

Cancer
Stroke
Myocardial Infarction
Renal Failure
Paralysis
Coronary Artery Surgery
Major Organ Transplant
Blindness
Heart Valve Replacement
Surgery for Diseases of the Aorta
Multiple Sclerosis
Aplastic Anemia
Benign Brain Tumor
End-Stage Lung Disease
Major Head Trauma
Complete Deafness
Loss of Limbs
Loss of Speech

#### Terms and Conditions
To be eligible for the cashback, the minimum quarterly transaction amount has to be BDT 30,000. 
2% cashback offer is only applicable on offline POS transactions at the groceries outlets mentioned above. 
The cashback facilities are not applicable for transactions made in MFS/Digital wallets, Fuel stations, Government fees and also for ATM withdrawals.
Polygon Max card issues against Foreign Currency (FC) Account, Resident Foreign Currency Deposit (RFCD) and Export Retention Quota (ERQ) Account and any such accounts are not eligible for cashback or interest-earning facilities. 
The accumulated cashback amount will be credited to your account at the end of each quarter.  
Each Polygon Max cardmember can earn a maximum amount equivalent to BDT 2,000 cashback per quarter 
15% VAT is applicable on payment of specific services as per guidelines of Bangladesh Bank. 
The additional interest amount earned in your savings account will be credited as per the general guideline of your bank account. 
To perform foreign currency transactions, Polygon Max cardmember will require to endorse his/her passport with Polygon Max card against the travel quota limit (maximum USD 12,000 per calendar year*). 
An endorsement can be performed at any Polygon Bank Authorized Dealer (AD) branch, Service Center.

Polygon Max cardmembers need to place an e-commerce-enabled request by calling [Polygon Bank hotline] or visiting the service center or web portal ([Polygon Bank website]) 

Polygon Max cardmember will be allowed to make online transactions equal to or less than USD $300 each against a travel quota card for any legal purposes and more than USD $300 each for hotel and education purposes. 

In case of transaction/s with any e-commerce merchant/s whose delivery timeline of products or services is/are after the prepayment by Polygon Max cardmember (hereinafter referred to as “Prepay E-Commerce Merchant/s”), the Polygon Max cardmember acknowledges, agrees and undertakes to resolve and settle all the refund, chargeback, non-delivery, claim, dispute issues (collectively referred to as "Dispute") with the respective prepay e-commerce merchant/s. 

Polygon Max cardmember acknowledges that we, the bank, shall not be liable for any such disputes of the Polygon Max cardmembers with the prepay e-commerce merchant/s and agrees to indemnify us, and keep us, the bank harmless against all losses, claims and expenses that we may incur due to any dispute between Polygon Max cardmember and prepay e-commerce merchant/s. 

Polygon Max cardmember acknowledges and agrees that the bank reserve the right to impose temporary/permanent restrictions on any type of transaction and/or with prepay e-commerce merchant/s.

#### Eligibility
Entry Age: Minimum 18 years to maximum 65 years
Exit Age: At Maturity, age of the policyholder can’t exceed 70 years
The policy applicant must have own, valid and regular income

#### Parameters
Tenor/Term: Minimum 5 years to maximum 52 years
Premium Mode: Monthly
Monthly Premium: Minimum of BDT 2000/-

#### Policy Benefit
At Maturity:

Upon maturity of the insurance plan, the policyholder will get the maturity benefit which includes the full sum assured along with the earned bonus.

At Death:

If the policyholder dies while the plan is in force before the expiry of the insurance plan, then the full sum assured plus the earned bonus shall be paid to the policyholder’s nominee.
However, only for Guardian Sanchay Gold policies, if the policyholder dies due to accidental death while the plan is in force before the expiry of the plan, then the nominee of the policyholder will get double the full sum assured along with the earned bonus.

Loan Facility:

Up to 90% of the net cash surrender/surrender value can be taken as insurance-loan.

Income Tax Rebate:

The insured customer will get an income tax rebate on the premium paid as per the income tax policy of the government.

Insurance claim money is completely tax free.

#### Applicable Riders
Double indemnity and accidental benefits equivalent to Sum Assured

Health Insurance

Hospitalization benefits up to BDT 5,00,000/- [Five Lac] for self and every family member [Spouse and Child] individually.

Critical Illness Insurance

18 Critical Illness Coverage for self, up to BDT 10,00,000/- [Ten Lac].

#### Mandatory Documents
Copy of the NID of the applicant
1 color passport-size photo of the applicant
Copy NID of the proposed nominee
1 color passport-size photo of the proposed nominee
Proof of income of the applicant, depending on the sum assured (will be required if sum assured crosses 25 Lacs)
Copy of the NID of the legal guardian where the proposed nominee is minor
1 color passport-size photo of the legal guardian where the proposed nominee is minor

#### Eligibility
Entry Age: Minimum 20 years to maximum 53 years
Exit Age: At Maturity, age of the policyholder can’t exceed 65 years

#### Parameters
Tenor/Term: 12, 16, 20, and 24 years
Policy Amount: Minimum of BDT 1,00,000/-
Premium Mode: Yearly, Half-yearly, Quarterly and Monthly
Partial Maturity: Applicable: 20%, 20%, 20% and 40%

#### Policy Benefits
At Maturity:

The sum assured will be paid in four installments, with earned bonus/profit, Three before the maturity of the insurance plan and the fourth at the end of the plan.
In the first phase, 20% of the basic sum assured, one - fourth of the maturity period.
In the second phase, next 20% of the basic sum assured, half of the maturity period
Third phase, next 20% of the basic sum assured, three-fourths of the maturity period
In the fourth phase [end], the remaining 40% of the basic sum assured and the bonus/profit will be paid.

At Death:

Upon receiving the first, second or third pay-out, if the policyholder dies before the expiry of the plan, then the nominee will be provided with the full sum assured along with the earned bonus.

Loan Facility:

Income Tax Rebate:

#### Applicable Riders
Permanent disability and accidental benefits for self, up to BDT 10,00,000/- [Ten Lac].

Health Insurance

Critical Illness Insurance

#### Parameters
Tenor/Term: Minimum 10 years to maximum 25 years

#### Policy Benefits
At Maturity:

Upon maturity, the entire sum assured will be paid to the nominated legal guardian with the earned bonus.

At Death:

Policyholder:
If the parent/policyholder dies while the plan is in force and prior to maturity, then the insured child will receive the following benefits:
15% of the sum assured will be paid to the designated legal guardian
1% of the sum assured amount will be paid to the legal guardian every month [stipend] till the expiry of the plan
All future premiums will be waived
 
Child:
If the child dies while the plan is in force and prior the maturity of the plan, then the parent/policyholder of the insured child will have the following benefits:
Up to 6 months: 25% of the Sum Assured;
More than 6 months but less than 12 months: 50% of the Sum Assured;
More than 12 months but less than 24 months: 75% of the Sum Assured;
More than 24 months: 100% of the Sum Assured

Income Tax Rebate:

#### Policy Benefits
At Maturity:

Upon maturity of the insurance plan, the policyholder will get survival benefi­t which includes the entire sum assured along with the earned bonus/pro­fit.

Surrender Value and Loan Benefi­t: Upon payment of at least 2 [Two] years of premium, a policy achieves Surrender Value. There is option for Policyholder to avail Policy Loan up to a maximum of 90% of the Surrender Value

Paid-up Option: The policy achieves paid-up status after paying at least 2 [Two] years of premium, after that the policy may be converted to a reduced Sum Assured upon request of the policyholder.

Tenor/Term: Minimum 5 years to maximum 40 years
Pension Receiving Age: Minimum 50 years
Yearly Pension: Minimum of BDT 24,000/-

#### Policy Benefits
At Maturity:

Upon maturity of the plan or at the retirement age [set by the policyholder], the policyholder will get the set monthly pension for his/her whole life.

At Death:

Policyholder:
If the policyholder dies while the plan is in force before the expiry of the insurance plan, the policyholder’s nominee will be paid 10 [Ten] times the set annual pension.
Pension Recipient:
If the pension recipient dies within the first 10 [Ten] years of the pension period, the monthly pension will be paid to the nominee of the pension recipient for the outstanding period; the outstanding period being the time remaining from the completion of the first 10 [Ten] year tenure.

Income Tax Rebate:

The policy Holder will get benefit of 15% income tax rebate on the premium paid every year as per the income tax policy of the government.
Death and living claim amount is completely tax free.
Paid up Value: After continuing the policy at least two years, the policy acquires Paid up value. Paid up value is payable with accrued bonus at the end of policy term.
Automatic Policy Loan (APL): If the Policy Holder misses a premium payment, the APL features automatically laps into policy holder’s policy’s cash value to cover the premium preventing policy from lapsing (T&C applicable).
Surrender Value: The Policy Holder can apply for the surrender value of the insurance policy at any time after paying at least 2 years of premium.
Karje Hasana: Upto 90% of the net cash surrender/surrender value can be taken as Karje Hasana (Interest-free Insurance Loan).

#### Features
The principle objective of this plan is to provide financial support for post-retirement life. The sum assured will be equivalent to the ten times of the annual pension payable over 10 years of pension payment term. Upon the survival of the policy holder at the end of the pension payment term, he or she will receive pension until death. If the policyholder dies before the commencement of the pension, the nominated beneficiary will get the sum assured either in the form of lump sum payment or as pension depending on the preference of the nominee. If the policy holder become permanently disable during the term of the policy, he or she would not have to pay any more premium but the policy will remain in-force. 50% of pension may be surrendered in lieu of cash benefit at the commencement of pension or within the first year of pension.

#### Eligibility
Entry Age: Minimum 18 years to maximum 50 years Exit Age: At maturity, age of the policyholder can’t exceed 60 years. The policy applicant must have own, valid & regular income.

#### Parameters
Tenor/Term: 10 or 20 Years Premium Mode: Monthly, Quarterly, Half-yearly & Yearly Minimum installment is BDT 5000

#### Policy Benefit
At Maturity: Upon maturity of the plan or at the retirement age [set by the policyholder], the policyholder will get the set monthly pension for his/her whole life. At Death: : If the policyholder dies while the plan is in force before the expiry of the insurance plan, the policyholder’s nominee will be paid 10 [Ten] times the set annual pension. Pension Recipient: If the pension recipient dies within the first 10 [Ten] years of the pension period, the monthly pension will be paid to the nominee of the pension recipient for the outstanding period; the outstanding period being the time remaining from the completion of the first 10 [Ten] year tenure. Loan Facility: Up to 90% of the net cash surrender/surrender value can be taken as insurance-loan. Income Tax Rebate: The insured customer will get an income tax rebate on the premium paid as per the income tax policy of the government. Insurance claim money is completely tax free.

#### Applicable Riders
You can choose one or more rider benefits to add your basic life insurance plan ADB – Accidental Death Benefit HI – Hospitalization Insurance MDB – Major Disease Benefit

#### Mandatory Documents
One copy passport size photo of the applicant and the child NID/ Passport/ Birth Certificate of the Policy Owner and nominee Customer need analysis form Standing Proof of income (salary certificate, trade license) Copy of the NID and 1 color passport-size photo of the legal guardian where the proposed nominee is minor         Insurance company may ask for additional documents

#### Benefits
ADB- Accidental Death Benefit

If the insured dies due to an external accident, in addition to the sum insured payable on the natural death of the insured, an amount equal to the rider amount is paid as per the contract. The maximum risk amount of this ADB is 20 lakh taka and the premium rate is 1.25 taka per thousand BDT.

 

PDAB-Permanent Disability & Accidental Benefit

This supplementary insurance provides financial assistance to the insured in case of permanent disability due to accident. If the insured becomes unable to carry out any profession or occupation due to permanent physical disability, a 'supplementary' benefit is provided at the rate of 10% of the main insurance amount every year for a maximum of 10 years.

In case of permanent complete disability, the premium is waived. In case of permanent partial/complete disability of any part of the body, compensation is given as per the schedule. However, compensation is not given for any disability that has already occurred. The premium of this supplementary insurance is only 3.50 taka for every thousand taka of the sum insured. The maximum sum insured of this supplementary insurance is 20 lakh taka and the age at maturity will be 60 years.

 

HI-Hospital Insurance

Under this supplementary insurance scheme, a maximum of 20 percent of the basic sum insured is paid for actual expenses incurred during hospitalization. The maximum amount of this claim is 5 lakh taka. If the insured wishes, his wife or husband and children can take advantage of this insurance simultaneously.

 

MDB 25%- Major Decease Benefit 25%

The aim of this scheme is to reduce the financial and mental burden of any serious illness. If the insured is affected by any of the 7 specified serious illnesses, 25% of the sum assured is paid immediately. The remaining 75% is paid at the time of claim settlement as usual. The maximum sum assured under this scheme is 20 lakh taka. The illnesses for which the sum assured is paid partially – Heart attack, Stroke, Coronary artery surgery, Cancer, Kidney failure, Multiple Sclerosis and Paralysis. The maximum age limit is 60 years.

 

MDB 50%- Major Decease Benefit 50%

The maximum sum insured in this plan is 10 lakh taka. The maximum age limit is 60 years. 8 diseases.  Heart attack, Stroke, Coronary artery surgery, Cancer, Kidney failure, Multiple Sclerosis, Paralysis and Major organ transplantation.

 

FIR- Family Income Rider

If the insured dies during the insurance period, 25% of the principal sum assured is paid immediately along with the bonus earned till the date of death. The remaining 75% is paid at the end of the term. In addition, 10% of the principal sum assured is paid to the family as an allowance on a monthly basis till the maturity. The term can be 10, 15 or 20 years. The maximum age limit is 60 years.

#### Parameter
Tenure: 1 year

#### Benefits of Polygon Bank's Custodial Services
It enables
NRBs to invest in the Bangladeshi Stock Market as well as IPOs (Initial Public Offerings) from anywhere around the world
Foreign individuals and foreign institutional investors to trade in Bangladesh Stock Market from anywhere around the globe
It holds the investors’ funds and shares/stocks in safe custody
Facilitates buy and sell of securities/stocks/shares through brokerage houses
Lodge IPO and Rights Share applications on behalf of the clients upon request
Collates information on the clients’ income from their investments in the stock market
Provides regular reporting of all activities along with managing transactions on behalf of the clients
Performs required foreign exchange transactions and also manages funds in NITA
Facilitates easy repatriation of the investors’ funds i.e. capital or principal investment, dividend and capital gains to the clients’ overseas bank accounts based on their instructions

Transfers from Foreign Currency accounts

Sale Proceeds of Shares
Dividends & Sale Proceeds from Bonus shares

All Bangladeshi nationals i.e. Bangladeshi passport holders who are residing/working abroad
Foreign passport holders with Bangladeshi origin
Foreign individuals
Foreign institutions

Open a Foreign Currency account with Polygon Bank to remit foreign currency to your account
Open a NITA (Non-Resident Investor's Taka Account) with Polygon Bank to make financial transactions for buying and selling of shares and also for receiving permissible deposits to NITA accounts
Open a BO (Beneficiary Owners) Account with Polygon Bank to hold stock/shares purchased by you
Open a Trading Account with a Brokerage House to facilitate trading of shares/stocks at the stock exchange. Polygon Bank will facilitate in opening this account

#### Facility
Polygon Bank Limited has 132 online branches across the country, Besides, the Bank has a strong remittance network with other major banks of the country. Therefore, wherever your account is, we are able to send your money instantly.

If you are a Polygon Bank account holder, then please visit any of our branches. Our Foreign remittance service personnel will be there to help you out. If you are not an account holder, then please open an account of your choice with us to receive your remittance at earliest convenience.

CBL understands the value of your precious time. That's why have made the payment procedure simple & easy. You have the privilege of enchashing the remitted money instantly from your branch counter without going through any hassle. That is to say, if you are an account holder of Polygon Bank, we can instantly credit the money to your account or pay cash to the receiver.

So, place your trust with Polygon Foreign Remittance Service. Send your money to your loved ones & experience peace of mind

#### Financial Literacy
For more information, please visit Bangladesh Bank Financial Literacy

#### Polygon Bank Initiatives
Financial Literacy Wing 

As the very first step towards this roadmap, the Polygon Bank has formed the Financial Literacy Wing(FLW). This wing will be at the forefront of the financial literacy initiatives by the bank and be responsible to follow the Financial Literacy Guidelines by Bangladesh Bank. Some of the core responsibilities of the members of the wing are— administering the financial literacy programs and reporting to the supervising authority BB and others.

Financial Literacy Program

Polygon Bank’s Financial Literacy Wing (FLW) has developed its Financial Literacy Plan and conducts financial literacy programs across Bangladesh, engaging its branches, sub-branches, agent banking outlets, and unit offices along with social media to bring the most marginalized people of our society under financial inclusion.

Financial Literacy Day Celebration

Polygon Bank celebrates Financial Literacy Day every year on the first Monday of March, focusing on different thematic areas of financial literacy to promote awareness among the general public and encourage responsible financial behavior across communities.

Financial Literacy ToT Session

Before rolling out the Financial Literacy Programs, Polygon Bank organized Training of Trainers (ToT) sessions to equip trainers with the necessary knowledge and facilitation skills. The inaugural ToT session was held in 2023 with 40 participants from Agent Banking and Branch Banking.

#### Who can open a Mariners FCY Account?
A Bangladeshi citizen, minimum 18 years of age, employed as a seafarer by a foreign shipping company earning in foreign currency.

#### Can a seafarer working for a Bangladeshi shipping company open this account?
No. This account is only for Bangladeshi seafarers employed by foreign shipping companies.

#### Can a foreign national open this account?
No. Only Bangladeshi citizens are eligible.

#### What type of account is Mariners FCY Account?
It is an Individual Current Account maintained in USD.

#### What documents are required to open the account?
Recent passport photo, NID/Passport/Birth Certificate, Nominee ID and photo, Utility bill (within 6 months) or parent/spouse utility copy with proof, Seaman’s Book/SRB/CDC/Discharge Book, Employment proof with joining date or contract.

#### Is there any minimum opening balance?
No, there is no minimum balance requirement.

#### In which branches can the account be opened?
At any Polygon Bank branch or sub-branch across Bangladesh.

#### Is joint account allowed?
No, joint account is not permitted.

#### Can mandate be assigned?
Yes, mandate assignment is allowed

#### What funds can be credited into this account?
Self-earned remittance, wage earner remittance, and foreign currency brought into Bangladesh (above USD 10,000 must be declared via customs FMJ form).

#### What funds cannot be credited?
Foreign exchange earned from business or services rendered within Bangladesh.

#### Is there any deposit limit?
No, deposit is unlimited as per applicable regulations.

#### Can an existing FCY account be converted?
Yes, if eligible. Customer must submit: i) Seaman’s Book/SRB/CDC/Discharge Book ii) Employer/agent contract or letter confirming next joining or current engagement.

#### Is cheque book available?
Yes, 1st cheque book is free. Subsequent books are chargeable as per SOC.

#### Is debit card available?
Yes, International Polygon Mastercard Debit Card is available and lifetime free for FCY account holders.

#### Can the debit card be used abroad?
Yes, it can be used overseas.

#### Are intercity cash transactions chargeable?
No, intercity cash deposit and withdrawal are free.

#### Is PolygonTouch available?
Yes, PolygonTouch digital banking is available.

#### Is SMS Alert available?
Yes, SMS Alert service is free of charge.

#### Is account maintenance fee applicable?
No, account maintenance fee is fully waived.

#### Is this an interest-bearing account?
No. This account does not offer interest on the deposited balance.

#### Are there any locker benefits?
Yes. A 50% waiver on the first year’s locker charge is offered. However, locker facilities are subject to availability at the respective branch.

#### Is student file opening fee discounted?
Yes. 50% waiver on child’s student file opening fee applies.

#### Eligibility
18 years or above Bangladeshi mariners or seafarers working for foreign shipping companies and salary credited in FCY.

#### Required Document
Valid passport 
Seaman’s Book or Seafarer’s Record Book (SRB) or Discharge Book or Continuous Discharge Certificate (CDC) 
Two recent passport-size photographs
Nominee’s NID/Passport/Birth Certificate
One passport-sized photograph of nominee
Proof of income/employment: Letter from local agent/association/employer confirming next date of joining vessel or current work contract. 
E‑TIN certificate (where applicable)

#### Interest Earnings
Mariners’ FCY Account is non-interest bearing account

#### Know More About Google Pay?
Q. what’s the relationship between Wallet and Pay? Are they separate? 

Answer: There is no separate app called "Google Pay” available in Bangladesh. The cards stored in Google Wallet are used to make payments via Google Pay. In this way, Wallet and Pay complement each other — one stores your cards, and the other facilitates payment using those cards.

Q. Which Polygon Bank cards are supported on Google Wallet? 

Answer: Polygon Bank All Visa and MasterCard (Debit, Credit and Prepaid cards) can be added to Google Wallet.

Q: What features will be available on Google Wallet? 

Answer: Currently, only payment services at POS machines that are supported NFC wallet transactions.

Q. How can I add my Polygon Bank card to Google Wallet? 

Answer: Steps to add:

Open the Google Wallet app on your Android device.
Tap on “Add to Wallet” → “Payment card”.
Use the camera to scan your card or enter card details manually.
Follow the on-screen authentication process (OTP will be sent to your registered mobile number).
Once verified, the card will be added and ready to use.

Do not share OTP with any one. 

Q. What devices are compatible with Google Wallet? 

Answer: Any Android smartphone running Android 7.0 or later, with NFC capability.

Q. Do I need to internet connection to add card in Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes, Internet connection is required to add card in Google Wallet.

Q. Where can I use Google Wallet? 

Answer: You can use Google Wallet at POS machine available at merchant location (locally or internationally) that support NFC wallet transaction.

Q. How do I pay using Google Wallet in stores? 

 Answer:

Unlock your phone.
Hold it near the contactless payment terminal.
Wait for the confirmation checkmark or vibration, and you’re done!

You can tap to pay anywhere you see the contactless or Google Pay symbols and POS machine that support NFC wallet transaction

Q. Can I add multiple Polygon Bank MasterCard and Visa Cards to Google Wallet? 

Answer:  Yes, you can add multiple Polygon Bank MasterCard and Visa credit or debit cards to Google Wallet. Simply follow the “Add to Wallet” steps for each card. You can choose a default card anytime.

Note: All contactless payments will be made from the card set as the default.

Q: How can I set or remove a default card in Google Wallet? 

Answer: The default card is the one used automatically for contactless payments (tap-to-pay) at NFC wallet transaction enabled POS machines.

To set a default card in Google Wallet:

Open the Google Wallet app.
Tap on the card you want to set as default.
Tap "Details" or the three-dot menu (â‹®).
Select "Set as default for tap to pay."

To remove a default card:

You cannot remove a default card without first assigning another card as default. To do this:

Add another card to your wallet (if not already added).
Set the new card as default using the steps above.
Then tap on the previous card > tap the three-dot menu (â‹®) > select "Remove payment method."

Q. Can I add my Polygon Bank MasterCard or Visa Cards to multiple Google Wallet? 

Answer:  Yes, you can your MasterCard and Visa cards in multiple to Google Wallet.

Q. Is it secure to use my card through Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes. Google Wallet uses tokenization, meaning your actual card number is never shared with the merchant. Additionally, your phone’s security features (e.g., screen lock or biometrics) help ensure only you can authorize purchases.

Q. Do I need to unlock my phone to pay? 

Answer: Yes, for security reasons, the phone must be unlocked using a screen lock (PIN, fingerprint, or pattern) before payment can be made.

Q. Do I need to internet connection to pay using google pay? 

Answer: No, Internet connection is not required to pay at Store.

Q. Will I need to enter a PIN for transactions? 

Answer: As per Bangladesh Bank regulations, NFC transactions above BDT 5,000 will require the PIN to be entered at the POS terminal.

Q. Can I do transactions at QR through Google Wallet? 

Answer: No — you cannot use Google Wallet (or Google Pay) to make QR-code payments in Bangladesh

Q. Can I use Google Wallet for online or in-app purchases? 

Answer: Currently, Polygon Bank Visa and Mastercard cards added to Google Wallet can be used for in-store (POS) contactless payments only. Online and in-app usage support will be communicated if enabled in future.

Q. What should I do if I lose my phone? 

Answer: If your phone is lost or stolen:

Call Polygon Bank’s 24/7 Contact Center at [Polygon Bank hotline] or [Polygon Bank hotline] (from abroad) to block your card.
Use Find My Device (https://www.google.com/android/find) to remotely lock or erase your device.

Q. Will I still earn rewards/benefits if I pay via Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes, all rewards/cashback/benefits (if any) associated with your Polygon Bank Visa or Mastercard will remain unchanged when you pay using Google Wallet.

Q.  What if I face an issue adding my card or during a transaction? 

Answer: Please contact Polygon Bank’s 24/7 Contact Center at:
[Polygon Bank hotline] (within Bangladesh)
[Polygon Bank hotline] (from abroad)

Q. Is there any charge for using Google Wallet? 

Answer: No. Polygon Bank does not charge any fee for enabling or using your card through Google Wallet. Standard card fees and interest (if applicable) remain unchanged.

Q. Can I remove my card from Google Wallet? 

Answer: Yes. Open the Google Wallet app → Select your card → Tap the three-dot menu → Choose Remove.

Q. How do I remove my old card from Google Wallet if I’ve replaced or renewed it? 

Answer: 

Open the Google Wallet app.
Select the old/expired card.
Tap the three-dot menu (or “More” option) and choose Remove Payment Method (or a similar option).
Add your new card by tapping Add to Wallet and following the prompts.

Q. What if my Polygon Bank Card is lost, stolen, or damaged and I receive a replacement card? 

Answer: 

Lost or Stolen Card: Once you report your card lost/stolen and receive a new Polygon Bank card, you must add this new card to Google Wallet. The old card is no longer valid, so you should remove it from your Google Wallet app.
Damaged Card: If you requested a replacement due to damage, follow the same steps—remove the old card from Google Wallet and add the new card once you receive it.

Q: What should I do if I find my card or mobile Device has been lost / stolen or feel my card or mobile Device data has been compromised? 

Answer:  Call [Polygon Bank hotline] immediately to notify about the lost/stolen card incident. Following your notification, the bank will restrict your card from any unauthorized transaction. However, the bank shall not take responsibility of an unauthorized transaction if it has happened prior your notification.

Q: What should I do if find a transaction SMS alert/call that has not been made by me? 

Answer:: Call us immediately at [Polygon Bank hotline] and lodge a complaint regarding this.

Q: Whom should I contact for any sort of transaction dispute issue? 

Answer:: Call at [Polygon Bank hotline] for any sort of cards dispute related queries

Q: My phone is Samsung – what NFC setting should I use to make Google Pay work? 

Answer:: For Samsung mobile users, please ensure the following settings for Google Pay to work properly:

Set Google Wallet as the default NFC payment app. Go to Settings → Connections → NFC and contactless payments → Select Google Wallet as the default payment application. 

Q: I can’t add my card to Google Wallet. What should I do? 

Answer: Some users may be unable to add their cards due to a device risk score issue, which means the device doesn’t meet Google’s security requirements. Please ensure the following:

Your phone is running Android 7.0 or higher. The device is Play Protect certified (not rooted or running a custom ROM). A screen lock (PIN, Pattern, or Fingerprint) is enabled. You are using an official version of Google Play Services. If your device still isn’t eligible, you may try adding the card using another compatible device. 

Q: I’ve added my card, but Tap and Pay isn’t working. What should I check? 

Answer: Please ensure the following if Tap and Pay is not functioning:

NFC is turned on in your phone’s settings. Google Wallet is set as the default Tap & Pay app. Your card is active and not expired. You are tapping on a contactless-enabled POS terminal. Your phone screen is on and unlocked at the time of payment. 

Q: Can I avail the Buy 1 Get 1 Free buffet offer when paying with Google Pay? 

Answer: No, currently the Buy 1 Get 1 Free buffet offer is not applicable for payments made through Google Pay. To enjoy this offer, please use your eligible physical Polygon Bank credit card directly at the participating hotel or restaurant.

#### What is Google Wallet?
- Google Wallet is a digital storage solution where user can securely store your bank cards, transit passes, and more. Its primary function is to store data — not to make payments directly.

#### What is Google Pay?
- Google Pay is a payment service that allows user to complete transactions using Google’s ecosystem. It works across various Google services such as the Play Store, Wallet, and more.

#### Frequently Asked Questions
Q1. What is Zero Charge Savings account?
Zero Charge Savings Account is a Savings deposit account in local currency with a minimum Account Opening Balance of BDT 500,000.

Q2. What are the benefits of opening Zero Charge Savings account?
The following fees and charges are waived for the account holders:

Q3. Who can open Zero Charge Savings account?
Bangladeshi citizens over the age of 18 may open the account.

Q4. What is the account type of Zero Charge Savings account?
It is an interest-bearing deposit account. (GFET Chapter 13 Section III, Para 25)

Q5. Can a customer open a joint Zero Charge Savings account?
Yes. The customer may open a joint account. 

Q6. Can an existing savings account holder convert to a Zero Charge Savings account?
Yes, an existing savings account holder may convert to a Zero Charge Savings account provided that their account has been open for 12 months or longer and has a 12- month average balance of BDT 500,000.

Q7. Is there any minimum balance threshold for this account?
Yes, a client has to maintain a minimum 6-month average balance of BDT 500,000 in order to enjoy the benefits free of charge/with waiver.

#### FAQ
Q1. Who can open a Business Solution Account?
Any non-individual customer such as businesses, institutions, organizations, and professional entities eligible under Polygon Bank’s non-individual account opening requirements.

Q2. Is this account available for individuals?
No. It is strictly for non-individual entities.

Q3. What type of account is this?
It is an interest-bearing transactional current account designed for non-individual customers.

Q4. What currency is this account maintained in?
The account is maintained in BDT (Bangladeshi Taka).

Q5. Can an entity open more than one Business Solution Account?
No. Each entity can open only one Business Solution Account.

Q6. Is joint ownership allowed?
No. Joint ownership is not applicable for non-individual accounts.

Q7. Where can a customer open this account?
This account can be opened at any branch or sub-branch of Polygon Bank across the country.

Q8. What documents are required to open this account?
Please see the details here.

Q9. How is the account operated?
The account is operated by the authorized signatory(ies) of the entity as per board resolution, mandate, partnership letter, or equivalent authorization documents.

Q10. If a customer already maintains a non-individual Current, Easy, or Easy Plus Account with Polygon Bank, can it be converted to a Business Solution Account?
Yes. Existing non-individual accounts may be converted into a Business Solution Account, subject to fulfilling all required documentation and product conditions.

Q11. Is there any limit on deposits?
No. Deposit amount is unlimited, in line with Bangladesh Bank regulations.

Q12. What is the minimum opening balance?
BDT 50,000.

Q13. What balance is required to earn interest?
Interest is earned when the daily End-of-Day balance is BDT 50,000 or above.

Q14. How is interest calculated?
Interest is calculated based on the daily End-of-Day balance.

Q15. When is interest paid?
Interest is paid on a monthly basis.

Q16. What is the interest rate?
Interest rates are slab-based, approved by ALCO, and updated on Polygon Bank’s website.

Q17. Is a cheque book available?
Yes. The first cheque book is free. Additional cheque books are free if the last six months’ monthly average balance was BDT 50,000 or above; otherwise, SOC charges apply.

Q18. Is a debit card available?
Yes, but only for proprietorship concerns.

Q19. What are the debit card charges?
All debit card-related charges follow the Schedule of Charges (SOC).

Q20. Is PolygonTouch Digital Banking available?

Q21. Is PolygonCorp available?
Yes. PolygonCorp access is available. To know more about PolygonCorp click here

Q22. Is SMS Alert service available?
Yes. SMS Alert service is available, and charges (if applicable) follow the Schedule of Charges.

Q23. Is an e-statement available?
Yes. An e-statement is available.

Q24. Is customer support available?
Yes. Customer support is available.

Q25. Is account maintenance fee applicable?
Yes. If the customer maintains a monthly average balance of BDT 50,000 or above during the last six months, no account maintenance fee will be charged. Otherwise, an account maintenance fee of BDT 500 will be charged on a half-yearly basis.

Q26. Are intercity cash transactions free?
Yes. Intercity cash deposits and withdrawals are free.

Q27. What is the account closing fee?
BDT 300.

Q28. Is excise duty applicable?
Yes. Excise duty is applied as per prevailing government regulations.

Q29. Is source tax applicable?
Yes. Source tax is deducted as per prevailing tax regulations.

#### Import Solutions
Simplify your purchasing process and enhance your creditworthiness with our robust import finance suite.

Letters of Credit (LC): Issuance of Sight, Deferred/Usance, and Back-to-Back LCs to ensure timely payment and secure delivery.
Import Financing: Specialized credit facilities, including Trust Receipts and Import Loan options, to manage payment obligations after shipment.
Trade Services: Efficient handling of Import Bills for Collection and issuance of Shipping Guarantees to facilitate prompt cargo clearance.
Guarantees & Bonds: Secure your contractual commitments with Bid Bonds, Performance Bonds, and Advance Payment Guarantees.

#### Export Solutions
Accelerate your cash flow and minimize risk when selling internationally.

LC Handling: Comprehensive services including Export LC Advising, Confirmation, and expert Document Checking and Negotiation to ensure fast, error-free processing.
Export Financing: Bridge the gap between production and payment with Pre-shipment (Packing Credit) and Post-shipment financing, alongside Export Invoice Financing.
Export Collections: Streamlined processing of export bills for collection.
Advisory & Support: Professional document preparation assistance to ensure compliance with international trade standards.

#### Why Choose Polygon Bank?
Full Spectrum Trade Instruments: We support your business needs with a robust range of instruments, including Import LCs, Export LCs, Back-to-Back LCs, and guarantees. Our team provides tailored advice to structure these instruments to suit your specific commodity or industrial requirements.
Streamlined Digital Execution via Polygon IMPEX: We accelerate your trade cycle through Polygon IMPEX, our specialized trade finance platform. This digital ecosystem allows your team to initiate, track, and manage all trade applications, LC issuance, and document presentation 24/7. 
Risk Mitigation & Advisory: We protect your cash flow through sophisticated bill discounting and collections management. Our experts work closely with you to mitigate counterparty and country risk, ensuring that every transaction—whether local or cross-border—meets international standards and Bangladesh Bank compliance protocols.

Direct access to a curated network of top-tier legal firms specializing in FDI and corporate law.

#### Why Choose Polygon Bank
Specialized Regional Expertise: We go beyond traditional banking by offering tailored support through dedicated regional desks. Our China Desk is fully operational and serves as a trusted partner for major enterprises, while our Japan, Korea, and Middle East desks are currently in development to provide the cultural and linguistic nuances necessary to navigate your specific business landscape with confidence.
Proven Gateway for Global Capital: As a premier financial institution in Bangladesh, we serve as the trusted bridge for international investors. Our deep integration with the local regulatory environment ensures your market entry is streamlined, compliant, and efficient from day one.
End-to-End Investment Lifecycle Support: We provide full-fledged financial solutions that span the entire investment journey—from pre-incorporation advisory and entity setup to complex structured finance and offshore banking. With Polygon Bank, you have a single, robust partner capable of managing the full spectrum of your operational and financial needs in Bangladesh.

#### Factoring Instant Liquidity against Receivables
Suppliers can assign their outstanding invoices to the bank to receive immediate funding. This converts credit sales into cash, eliminating the wait for payment terms.

#### Work Order Financing Bridging the Procurement Gap
Get up to 85% of your invoice value funded upon delivery of goods. This facility ensures suppliers have the capital to fulfill orders, with the buyer settling the dues on the maturity date.

#### Reverse Factoring Buyer-Led Supplier Credit
Leverage the credit strength of the Corporate Buyer to provide suppliers with early payment. This strengthens supplier relationships and secures your production line.

#### Ecosystem Benefits
For the Anchor (Buyer)

	

For the Supplier (Seller)

Extended payment terms without straining suppliers.

	

Access to low-cost funding based on the buyer's credit.

Improved supply chain stability and loyalty.

	

Faster conversion of receivables into cash.

Strengthened balance sheet and cash forecasting.

	

Elimination of the need for hard collateral.

Flexible & Simplified Access: Enjoy a streamlined onboarding process with minimal documentation, including unique flexibility for small-ticket LCs and niche SME requirements.

Digital Payments: Execute thousands of salary and vendor payments instantly via BEFTN, RTGS, NPSB, bKash, and Polygon Bank transfers, achieving automation with minimal manual intervention.

Strong Governance: Ensure strong internal control by operating PolygonCorp on a structured Maker–Checker–Approver workflow aligned with your Board‑approved mandate structure.

### Financial Literacy

Financial literacy is the awareness, knowledge, skills, and behavior needed to make sound financial decisions and it is essential for managing money effectively and achieving financial well-being.
Financial literacy has become vital for sustainable financial inclusion
As Bangladesh advances in Digital Financial Services DFS, financial literacy has become vital for sustainable financial inclusionan important goal under the UN SDGs, Bangladesh Vision 2041, and the National Financial Inclusion Strategy NFIS-B.
Polygon Bank is committed to building a financially literate and inclusive society
Aligned with Bangladesh Banks vision, Polygon Bank is committed to building a financially literate and inclusive society. Through continuous efforts to promote knowledge, inclusion, and empowerment, the bank believes financial literacy is key to Bangladeshs long-term economic growth
Financial literacy is needed to make sound financial decisions
Photo Gallery

#### Financial Literacy
For more information, please visit Bangladesh Bank Financial Literacy

#### Polygon Bank Initiatives
Financial Literacy Wing 

As the very first step towards this roadmap, the Polygon Bank has formed the Financial Literacy Wing(FLW). This wing will be at the forefront of the financial literacy initiatives by the bank and be responsible to follow the Financial Literacy Guidelines by Bangladesh Bank. Some of the core responsibilities of the members of the wing are— administering the financial literacy programs and reporting to the supervising authority BB and others.

Financial Literacy Program

Polygon Bank’s Financial Literacy Wing (FLW) has developed its Financial Literacy Plan and conducts financial literacy programs across Bangladesh, engaging its branches, sub-branches, agent banking outlets, and unit offices along with social media to bring the most marginalized people of our society under financial inclusion.

Financial Literacy Day Celebration

Polygon Bank celebrates Financial Literacy Day every year on the first Monday of March, focusing on different thematic areas of financial literacy to promote awareness among the general public and encourage responsible financial behavior across communities.

Financial Literacy ToT Session

Before rolling out the Financial Literacy Programs, Polygon Bank organized Training of Trainers (ToT) sessions to equip trainers with the necessary knowledge and facilitation skills. The inaugural ToT session was held in 2023 with 40 participants from Agent Banking and Branch Banking.

### Customer service and complaints management cells

How customers raise a complaint and how it is handled.

```text
Customer Service & Complaints Management Cells  
 
Page 1 of 4 
We, at Polygon Bank, believe in offering enjoyable experiences, which is one of the core elements of our bank's vision. Our 
constant endeavor is to make you happy with our services and support. But in the real world, however much we try, 
you may still have co ncerns over our services which you may also want to share with us. We are always look forward 
for any service and feedback  
We, in fact, encourage you to share, so that we are aware and we can resolve the issue. Any issue brought to our 
attention will be tr eated in a confidential manner and will be discussed only with the people who need to be involved.  
Please follow the following steps to resolve your concern:  
 
Step -1: Contact with the concerned official of your Branch or our Customer Service Centers  
Please  refer your problem to the concerned official of your branch first who deals with your case on how you would like 
the problem to be resolved.  
Polygon Bank  Branches  
To find out the address and contact details of your branch, please visit our website [Polygon Bank website] or 
click on this  link: [Polygon Bank website] .  
Polygon Bank American Express Service Centers  
For Cardmembers, our Call Center is available 24 hours a day, 7 days a week to address your card related 
enquiries/complaints. Alternatively, you may visit any of our Polygon Bank American Express Service Centers to satisfy your 
needs. To find out the contact details of our Call Center and the address of our Polygon Bank American E xpress Service 
Centers, please visit our American Express Cards website https://www.americanexpress.com/  or click on this link: 
https://www.americanexpress.com/e n-bd/network/help/get -in-touch/  
 
Step -2: Contact Branch Level Customer Service & Complaints Management Desk (BLCS&CMD)  
If your complaint is not resolved at the desk level, then you may make a formal complaint to the Branch Manager as 
he/she is holding the Branch Level Customer Service & Complaints Management Desk (BLCS&CMD).  
Please collect a Complaint Lodgment Form  from the Branch Manager, write down your complaints in details and 
submit it to him. Alternatively,  you may call, fax, e -mail or send your own complaint letter to the Branch Manager  by 
post or hand delivery . 
Please note that you can lodge your complaint to the Branch Manager of any branch on any kind of product or service 
offered by o ur bank regardless of the branch/division at which you opened an account or the branch/division at which 
you conducted a financial activity or transaction.  
 
Step -3: Contact Zonal Customer Service & Complaints Management Cells (ZCS&CMC)  
If your complaint is  still unresolved or you are still not satisfied, then you may submit your complaint to any of the 
following Zonal Customer Service & Complaints Management Cells (ZCS&CMC):  
Dial our 24 -hours customer service at [Polygon Bank hotline]  (locally) or [Polygon Bank hotline]  (from overs eas).
```
